In this work, we introduce a \emph{dynamic cluster-based sampling approach (DynamiCS)} that downsamples large clusters of data and upsamples small ones. The approach is dynamic in that it applies sampling at each epoch. We first show the importance of dynamic sampling for VLM training. Then, we demonstrate the advantage of our cluster-scaling approach, which maintains the relative order of semantic clusters in the data and emphasizes the long-tail. This approach contrasts with current work, which focuses only on flattening the semantic distribution of the data. Our experiments show that DynamiCS reduces the computational cost of VLM training and provides a performance advantage for long-tail concepts.

</details>


### [119] [TripVVT: A Large-Scale Triplet Dataset and a Coarse-Mask Baseline for In-the-Wild Video Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27958)
*Dingbao Shao,Song Wu,Shenyi Wang,Ye Wang,Ziheng Tang,Fei Liu,Jiang Lin,Xinyu Chen,Qian Wang,Ying Tai,Jian Yang,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TripVVT-10K数据集、TripVVT框架和TripVVT-Bench基准，解决了视频虚拟试穿中数据稀缺和掩码使用不当的问题，实现了更鲁棒、更真实的野外视频虚拟试穿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法受限于大规模野外三元组数据稀缺以及掩码使用不当，导致模型性能有限，难以处理真实世界中的运动、遮挡和复杂场景。

Method: 1) 构建TripVVT-10K数据集，提供显式的视频级跨服装监督信号；2) 提出基于Diffusion Transformer的TripVVT框架，用简单稳定的人体掩码先验替代脆弱的服装掩码；3) 建立TripVVT-Bench基准，覆盖100个案例及多维度评估指标。

Result: 在视频质量、服装保真度、背景一致性和时间连贯性方面均优于现有学术和商业系统，且对野外视频的泛化能力显著提升。

Conclusion: TripVVT系列工作为可控、真实且时间稳定的视频虚拟试穿提供了坚实的数据和模型基础。

Abstract: Due to the scarcity of large-scale in-the-wild triplet data and the improper use of masks, the performance of video virtual try-on models remains limited. In this paper, we first introduce **TripVVT-10K**, the largest and most diverse in-the-wild triplet dataset to date, providing explicit video-level cross-garment supervision that existing video datasets lack. Built upon this resource, we develop **TripVVT**, a Diffusion Transformer-based framework that replaces fragile garment masks with a simple, stable human-mask prior, enabling reliable background preservation while remaining robust to real-world motion, occlusion, and cluttered scenes. To support comprehensive evaluation, we further establish **TripVVT-Bench**, a 100-case benchmark covering diverse garments, complex environments, and multi-person scenarios, with metrics spanning video quality, try-on fidelity, background consistency, and temporal coherence. Compared to state-of-the-art academic and commercial systems, TripVVT achieves superior video quality and garment fidelity while markedly improving generalization to challenging in-the-wild videos. We publicly release the dataset and benchmark, which we believe provide a solid foundation for advancing controllable, realistic, and temporally stable video virtual try-on.

</details>


### [120] [ClimateVID -- Social Media Videos Analysis and Challenges Involved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27968)
*Shiqi Xu,Moritz Burmester,Katharina Prasse,Isaac Bravo,Stefanie Walter,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了视觉语言模型（VLM）在社交媒体短视频视觉主题检测中的零样本分类能力，并将聚类作为最小成本多割问题处理，发现ConvNeXt V2和DINOv2能生成有意义的聚类，但VLM目前无法检测气候变化相关类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字内容（特别是社交媒体短视频）的激增显著改变了公共 discourse 中话题的讨论和理解方式，因此需要推进自动化视觉主题检测方法。

Method: (1) 评估VideoChatGPT、PandaGPT、VideoLLava等VLM在零样本图像分类上的表现，并与基于帧的CLIP图像分类基线对比；(2) 将聚类视为最小成本多割问题，以无监督方式发现洞察模式。

Result: VLM目前无法检测气候变化特定类别；ConvNeXt V2和DINOv2均能产生有意义的聚类，其中DINOv2更关注风格差异和抽象类别，而ConvNeXt V2的聚类在更细粒度层面区分。

Conclusion: 研究提供了两种分析策略的广泛评估和实践指导，代码已开源。

Abstract: The pervasive growth of digital content, specifically short videos on social media platforms, has significantly altered how topics are discussed and understood in public discourse. In this work, we advance automated visual theme detection by assessing zero-shot and clustering capabilities on social media data. (1) We evaluated the capabilities of notable VLMs such as VideoChatGPT, PandaGPT, and VideoLLava using zero-shot image classification and compared their performance to the baseline provided by frame-wise CLIP image classification. (2) By treating clustering as a minimum cost multicut problem, we aim to uncover insightful patterns in an unsupervised manner. For both analysis strategies, we provide extensive evaluations and practical guidance to practitioners. While VLMs are currently not able to detect climate change specific classes, the clustering results are distinct visual frames. %Given that VLMs are not currently capable to grasp the climate change discourse, we focus the clustering evaluation of image embedding models. We find that both ConvNeXt V2 and DINOv2 produce meaningful clusters, with DINOv2 focusing more on style differences and abstract categories, while ConvNeXt V2 clusters differ in more fine-grained ways. Code available at https://github.com/KathPra/ClimateVID.git.

</details>


### [121] [FineState-Bench: Benchmarking State-Conditioned Grounding for Fine-grained GUI State Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27974)
*Fengxian Ji,Jingpu Yang,Zirui Song,Yuanxi Wang,Zhexuan Cui,Yuke Li,Qian Jiang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FineState-Bench基准，用于评估LVLMs在细粒度、状态条件化的GUI交互中的表现，包含2209个实例覆盖桌面/Web/移动端，并设计了四阶段诊断指标和可视化诊断助手VDA。实验表明当前模型精确状态成功率较低（平均22.8%），视觉定位是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLMs评估在GUI交互中存在覆盖范围有限、目标状态定义不精确、过度依赖最终任务成功率的缺陷，无法定位智能体失败的具体环节（如定位错误、交互错误还是状态设置错误）。

Method: 构建FineState-Bench（2209实例，4类交互族，23种UI组件，跨桌面/Web/移动三平台）；提出FineState-Metrics四阶段诊断流水线（SR@Loc, SR@Int, ES-SR@Loc, ES-SR@Int）；设计VDA（Visual Diagnostic Assistant）通过生成描述和边界框定位提示进行控制变量分析。

Result: 精确状态成功率ES-SR@Int在Web上最高为32.8%，跨平台平均仅22.8%；引入VDA定位提示后Gemini-2.5-Flash提升14.9个百分点的ES-SR@Int，表明视觉定位改进仍有很大空间，但整体精度仍不足以支持可靠的细粒度状态条件化交互。

Conclusion: FineState-Bench揭示了当前LVLMs在细粒度GUI状态条件化交互中的根本瓶颈在于视觉定位，为后续研究提供了诊断工具和明确改进方向。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of large vision-language models (LVLMs), fine-grained, state-conditioned GUI interaction remains challenging. Current evaluations offer limited coverage, imprecise target-state definitions, and an overreliance on final-task success, obscuring where and why agents fail. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{FineState-Bench}, a benchmark that evaluates whether an agent can correctly ground an instruction to the intended UI control and reach the exact target state. FineState-Bench comprises 2,209 instances across desktop, web, and mobile platforms, spanning four interaction families and 23 UI component types, with each instance explicitly specifying an exact target state for fine-grained state setting. We further propose \textit{FineState-Metrics}, a four-stage diagnostic pipeline with stage-wise success rates: Localization Success Rate (SR@Loc), Interaction Success Rate (SR@Int), Exact State Success Rate at Locate (ES-SR@Loc), and Exact State Success Rate at Interact (ES-SR@Int), and a plug-and-play \textit{Visual Diagnostic Assistant} (VDA) that generates a Description and a bounding-box Localization Hint to diagnose visual grounding reason via controlled w/ vs.\ w/o comparisons. On FineState-Bench, exact goal-state success remains low: ES-SR@Int peaks at 32.8\% on Web and 22.8\% on average across platforms. With VDA localization hints, Gemini-2.5-Flash gains +14.9 ES-SR@Int points, suggesting substantial headroom from improved visual grounding, yet overall accuracy is still insufficient for reliable fine-grained state-conditioned interaction \href{https://github.com/FengxianJi/FineState-Bench}{Github.}

</details>


### [122] [Echo-α: Large Agentic Multimodal Reasoning Model for Ultrasound Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28011)
*Jing Zhang,Wentao Jiang,Tao Huang,Zhiwei Wang,Jianxin Liu,Jian Chen,Ping Ye,Gang Wang,Zengmao Wang,Bo Du,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Echo-α是一个面向超声影像解读的智能多模态推理模型，通过“调用与推理”框架统一了专用检测器的强定位能力与大语言模型的弱推理能力。模型经九任务监督课程学习与序列强化学习优化，在肾脏和乳腺超声的多中心基准测试中，病灶定位（F1@0.5达56.73%/43.78%）和最终诊断（准确率74.90%/49.20%）均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声解读方法存在能力割裂：专用检测器定位精准但推理弱，多模态大语言模型推理灵活但专业性不足，缺乏能同时实现精准定位与临床推理的统一框架。

Method: 提出Echo-α模型，采用invoke-and-reason框架，协调器官专用检测器的输出并融合全局视觉上下文，将证据转化为接地气的诊断决策。通过九任务监督课程学习建立行为基础，再经序列强化学习按不同奖励权衡优化，得到Echo-α-Grounding（病灶锚定）和Echo-α-Diagnosis（最终诊断）两个变体。

Result: 在多中心肾脏/乳腺超声基准上，Echo-α-Grounding在跨中心测试集达到F1@0.5为56.73%/43.78%，Echo-α-Diagnosis总体准确率达74.90%/49.20%，均优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: 智能多模态推理可将专用检测器转化为可验证的临床证据，为构建更准确、可解释、可迁移的超声AI系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Ultrasound interpretation requires both precise lesion localization and holistic clinical reasoning, yet existing methods typically excel at only one of these capabilities: specialized detectors offer strong localization but limited reasoning, whereas multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide flexible reasoning but weak grounding in specialized medical domains. We present Echo-α, an agentic multimodal reasoning model for ultrasound interpretation that unifies these strengths within an invoke-and-reason framework. Echo-α is trained to coordinate organ-specific detector outputs, integrate them with global visual context, and convert the resulting evidence into grounded diagnostic decisions beyond detector-only inference. This behavior is established through a nine-task supervised curriculum and then refined by sequential reinforcement learning under different reward trade-offs, yielding Echo-α-Grounding for lesion anchoring and Echo-α-Diagnosis for final diagnosis. On multi-center renal and breast ultrasound benchmarks, Echo-α outperforms competitive baselines on both grounding and diagnosis. In particular, on cross-center test sets, Echo-α-Grounding attains 56.73%/43.78% F1@0.5 and Echo- α-Diagnosis reaches 74.90%/49.20% overall accuracy on renal/breast ultrasound. These results suggest that agentic multimodal reasoning can turn specialized detectors into verifiable clinical evidence, offering a practical route toward ultrasound AI systems that are more accurate, interpretable, and transferable. The repository is at https://github.com/MiliLab/Echo-Alpha.

</details>


### [123] [Faster 3D Gaussian Splatting Convergence via Structure-Aware Densification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28016)
*Linjie Lyu,Ayush Tewari,Jianchun Chen,Thomas Leimkühler,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构感知的3D高斯泼溅密集化框架，通过多尺度频率分析和各向异性分裂策略，在加速收敛的同时提升了高频区域的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3D高斯泼溅的自适应密度控制依赖屏幕空间位置梯度，无法区分几何错位和频率混叠，导致高频纹理过模糊或低效过度密集化。

Method: 结合结构张量与拉普拉斯尺度空间分析进行多尺度频率分析，估计每个像素的主频；定义每高斯每轴的频率违规度量η；基于η进行各向异性分裂（非均匀分裂因子）；引入多视角一致性准则聚合η观测值；提前并加速密集化过程。

Result: 在标准基准测试中取得了更优的重建质量，特别是在高频区域；显著加速收敛，跳过了基线方法所需的长迭代密集化阶段。

Conclusion: 通过显式比较高斯投影屏幕空间范围与待表示纹理的局部结构来决定是否细分，实现了更高效、更高质量的密集化。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a powerful scene representation for real-time novel-view synthesis. However, its standard adaptive density control relies on screen-space positional gradients, which do not distinguish between geometric misplacement and frequency aliasing, often leading to either over-blurred high-frequency textures or inefficient over-densification. We present a structure-aware densification framework. Our key insight is that the decision to subdivide a Gaussian should be driven by an explicit comparison between its projected screen-space extent and the local structure of the texture it seeks to represent. We introduce a multi-scale frequency analysis combining structure tensors with Laplacian scale space analysis to estimate the dominant frequency at each pixel, enabling robust supervision across varying texture scales. Based on this analysis, we define $η$, a per-Gaussian, per-axis frequency violation metric that indicates when a primitive may be under-resolving local texture details. Unlike methods that perform isotropic splitting (e.g., splitting each Gaussian into two smaller ones with uniform shape), our approach performs anisotropic splitting. For each axis with high $η$, we compute a split factor to better resolve the local frequency content. We further introduce a multiview consistency criterion that aggregates $η$ observations across multiple views. By performing densification early and faster, we skip the lengthy iterative densification phases required by baseline methods and achieve significantly faster convergence. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that our method also achieves superior reconstruction quality, particularly in high-frequency regions.

</details>


### [124] [TAFA-GSGC: Group-wise Scalable Point Cloud Geometry Compression with Progressive Residual Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28045)
*Xiumei Li,Alexander Kopte,André Kaup*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAFA-GSGC，一种可扩展学习的点云几何压缩框架，支持从单一码流和单一模型实现多质量解码，通过分层残差细化与通道组熵编码结合，并引入目标对齐特征聚合模块减少跨层冗余，在9个质量等级上实现单调质量提升，相比PCGCv2在D1/D2指标上分别节省4.99%和5.92%的BD-Rate。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型编解码器大多针对固定率失真点优化，实现率自适应需要重新编码或维护多个码流，成本高昂。为解决可扩展压缩中带宽自适应传输的需求，需要一种能从单一码流和单一模型实现多质量解码的点云几何压缩方法。

Method: 采用分层残差细化结构（layered residual refinement）结合通道组熵编码（channel-group entropy coding），并设计目标对齐特征聚合模块（Target-Aligned Feature Aggregation）来减少增强残差中的跨层冗余。

Result: 支持多达9个可解码质量等级，随子码流增加质量单调提升；相比PCGCv2，在D1指标上平均节省4.99% BD-Rate，在D2指标上平均节省5.92% BD-Rate，压缩效率相当或略优。

Conclusion: TAFA-GSGC实现了高效的可扩展点云几何压缩，在保持强压缩效率的同时，支持多质量解码，为带宽自适应传输提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Scalable compression is essential for bandwidth-adaptive transmission, yet most learned codecs are optimized for a fixed rate-distortion point, making rate adaptation costly due to re-encoding or maintaining multiple bitstreams. In this work, we propose TAFA-GSGC, a scalable learned point cloud geometry codec that enables multi-quality decoding from a single bitstream and a single trained model. TAFA-GSGC combines layered residual refinement with channel-group entropy coding, and introduces Target-Aligned Feature Aggregation module to reduce cross-layer redundancy in enhancement residuals. Our framework supports up to 9 decodable quality levels with monotonic quality improvement as more subbitstreams are received, while maintaining strong compression efficiency. Compared with the baseline PCGCv2, TAFA-GSGC attains comparable and slightly better RD performance, achieving average BD-Rate savings of -4.99% in D1 and -5.92% in D2.

</details>


### [125] [3D Reconstruction Techniques in the Manufacturing Domain: Applications, Research Opportunities and Use Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28064)
*Chialoon Cheng,Kaijun liu,Zhiyang Liu,Marcelo H Ang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了制造业中三维重建技术的演进与现状，涵盖传统方法与深度学习技术，通过分析106篇文献将重建技术分为数据采集、点云生成、后处理与应用三类，指出非接触方法（特别是结构光扫描和立体视觉）占主导地位，深度学习提升了精度与速度，主要应用为质量检测（40%），同时揭示了统一三维重建框架的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业中三维重建技术发展迅速，但缺乏统一框架来系统理解不同方法的优劣、应用场景及未来方向，尤其是传统方法与深度学习方法的融合趋势尚不明确。

Method: 对106篇近期出版物进行系统综述，将重建技术分类为数据采集、点云生成、后处理与应用三大类，按应用场景（设计开发、加工、工艺、装配、质量检测）进行统计分析。

Result: 非接触方法（结构光扫描、立体视觉）应用最广；47%的调查应用聚焦质量检测；深度学习在特征提取与匹配中提升了精度和速度；混合系统成为克服单一方法局限性的趋势。

Conclusion: 当前技术在受控环境下可达亚毫米级精度，但反射表面和动态环境仍是挑战；未来方向为融合多传感器与多种处理方法的混合系统，且亟需统一的三维重建框架。

Abstract: This comprehensive review examines the evolution and the current state of the art in three-dimensional (3D) reconstruction techniques in manufacturing applications. The analysis covers both traditional approaches and emerging deep learning methods, showing a critical research gap in unified 3d reconstruction frameworks. Through systematic review of 106 recent publications, we classify reconstruction techniques into three primary categories: data acquisition, point cloud generation, post-processing and applications. Non-contact methods, particularly structured light scanning and stereo vision, have shown significant adoption in manufacturing, with 47% of surveyed applications focusing on quality inspection. The integration of deep learning has enhanced reconstruction accuracy and processing speed, particularly in feature extraction and matching. Key applications span design and development (13%), machining (8%), process (17%), assembly (22%), and quality inspection (40%). While current technologies achieve sub-millimeter accuracy in controlled environments, challenges persist in handling reflective surfaces and dynamic environments. Our findings indicate a trend toward hybrid systems combining multiple sensor types and processing methods to overcome individual limitations. This survey provides a structured framework for understanding current capabilities and future directions in manufacturing-focused 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [126] [Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Object Detection on Edge Computing Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16808)
*Daghash K. Alqahtani,Aamir Cheema,Adel N. Toosi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在树莓派3/4/5（带/不带TPU）和Jetson Orin Nano等边缘设备上系统评估了YOLOv8、EfficientDet Lite、SSD系列目标检测模型的能耗、推理时间和mAP性能，发现低mAP模型（如SSD MobileNet V1）更节能高效，高mAP模型（如YOLOv8 Medium）通常能耗更高，Jetson Orin Nano在速度和能效方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主驾驶等现代应用需要在资源受限的边缘设备上部署深度学习算法进行实时图像视频处理，但目前对各类目标检测模型在边缘设备上的效率和性能缺乏深入理解。

Method: 选取YOLOv8（Nano, Small, Medium）、EfficientDet Lite（Lite0, Lite1, Lite2）和SSD（SSD MobileNet V1, SSDLite MobileDet）三类模型，部署在树莓派3/4/5（带/不带TPU加速器）和Jetson Orin Nano上，收集能耗、推理时间和mAP等关键性能指标。

Result: 低mAP模型（如SSD MobileNet V1）更节能且推理速度更快；高mAP模型（如YOLOv8 Medium）通常能耗更高、推理更慢，但使用TPU加速器时有例外；Jetson Orin Nano在速度和能效上表现最佳，尽管其空闲能耗最高。

Conclusion: 在边缘设备部署深度学习模型时需权衡精度、速度与能效三者关系，本文结果为从业者和研究者选择模型与设备提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Modern applications, such as autonomous vehicles, require deploying deep learning algorithms on resource-constrained edge devices for real-time image and video processing. However, there is limited understanding of the efficiency and performance of various object detection models on these devices. In this paper, we evaluate state-of-the-art object detection models, including YOLOv8 (Nano, Small, Medium), EfficientDet Lite (Lite0, Lite1, Lite2), and SSD (SSD MobileNet V1, SSDLite MobileDet). We deployed these models on popular edge devices like the Raspberry Pi 3, 4, and 5 with/without TPU accelerators, and Jetson Orin Nano, collecting key performance metrics such as energy consumption, inference time, and Mean Average Precision (mAP). Our findings highlight that lower mAP models such as SSD MobileNet V1 are more energy-efficient and faster in inference, whereas higher mAP models like YOLOv8 Medium generally consume more energy and have slower inference, though with exceptions when accelerators like TPUs are used. Among the edge devices, Jetson Orin Nano stands out as the fastest and most energy-efficient option for request handling, despite having the highest idle energy consumption. These results emphasize the need to balance accuracy, speed, and energy efficiency when deploying deep learning models on edge devices, offering valuable guidance for practitioners and researchers selecting models and devices for their applications.

</details>


### [127] [AesRM: Improving Video Aesthetics with Expert-Level Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28078)
*Yujin Han,Yujie Wei,Yefei He,Xinyu Liu,Tianle Li,Zichao Yu,Andi Han,Shiwei Zhang,Tingyu Weng,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视频美学层次化评估框架，将视频美学分解为视觉美学(VA)、视觉保真度(VF)和视觉合理性(VP)三个核心维度及15个细粒度标准，构建了大规模专家标注偏好数据集AesVideo-Bench，并训练了视频美学奖励模型AesRM（含Base和CoT两个版本），通过三阶段渐进训练和自一致性CoT合成等方法，显著提升了视频美学评估的准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成研究主要关注真实感，而实际应用（如电影制作）还需要和谐色彩、电影级光照等美学质量。同时，现有视觉美学研究多聚焦于图像且定义粗粒度（如"视觉愉悦性"），缺乏系统严格的评估体系。

Method: 1) 提出层次化学科框架，将视频美学分解为VA、VF、VP三个维度共15个细粒度标准；2) 构建AesVideo-Bench基准（约2500个视频对，含专家标注）；3) 训练AesRM-Base（直接预测偏好）和AesRM-CoT（生成符合15个标准的CoT）；4) 设计三阶段渐进训练：原子美学能力学习、冷启动对齐、GRPO优化；5) 提出基于自一致性的CoT合成方法和基于CoT的过程奖励机制。

Result: AesRM在多个美学基准上优于基线模型，具有更低的位置偏差和更强的鲁棒性。将Wan2.2与AesRM对齐后在美学质量上显著优于现有美学奖励模型。

Conclusion: 提出的层次化学科评估框架和AesRM模型能有效提升视频美学评估的准确性与可解释性，并能作为奖励信号指导视频生成模型的美学优化。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in photorealistic video generation, real-world applications such as filmmaking require video aesthetics, e.g., harmonious colors and cinematic lighting, beyond visual fidelity. Prior work on visual aesthetics largely focuses on images, often reducing aesthetics to coarse definitions, e.g., visual pleasure, without a rigorous and systematic evaluation. To improve video aesthetics, we propose a hierarchical rubric that decomposes video aesthetics into three core dimensions, Visual Aesthetics (VA), Visual Fidelity (VF), and Visual Plausibility (VP), with 15 fine-grained criteria, e.g., shot composition. This framework enables a large-scale expert-annotated preference dataset and an evaluation benchmark, AesVideo-Bench, containing about 2500 video pairs with expert annotations on VA, VF, and VP. We then build a family of Video Aesthetic Reward Models (AesRM): AesRM-Base, which directly predicts pairwise preferences on these dimensions to provide efficient post-training rewards, and AesRM-CoT, which additionally generates CoT aligned with all 15 criteria to improve assessment interpretability. Specifically, we train AesRM with a three-stage progressive scheme: (1) Atomic Aesthetic Capability Learning, which strengthens AesRM's recognition of fundamental aesthetic concepts, e.g., accurately identifying centered composition; (2) Cold-Start, aligning the model with structured reasoning protocols; and (3) GRPO, further improving evaluation accuracy. To enhance AesRM-CoT, we additionally propose self-consistency-based CoT synthesis to improve CoT quality and design CoT-based process rewards during GRPO. Extensive experiments show AesRM outperforms baselines on multiple aesthetics benchmarks and is more robust, with lower position bias. Finally, we align Wan2.2 with AesRM and observe clear aesthetic gains over existing aesthetic reward models.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Gaussian Bottlenecks: Topologically Aligned Encoding of Vision-Transformer Feature Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28122)
*Andrew Bond,Ilkin Umut Melanlioglu,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S$^2$VAE提出了一种几何优先的潜空间学习框架，通过使用Power Spherical分布乘积的变分自编码器，在强压缩下保持场景的3D几何结构（包括相机运动、深度和点云结构），在深度估计、相机姿态恢复和点云重建中优于传统高斯瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉世界建模系统虽然能生成看似合理的运动，但往往无法保持底层的3D几何结构和物理一致的相机动态。关键在于模型不仅受限于容量，更受限于用于编码几何结构的潜表示。

Method: 基于VGGT表示，提出一种新型VAE，使用Power Spherical潜变量分布的乘积（product of Power Spherical latent distributions），在瓶颈层显式强制超球面结构，以在强压缩下保持方向性和几何语义。

Result: 在深度估计、相机姿态恢复和点云重建任务中，几何对齐的超球面潜变量在强压缩条件下始终优于传统高斯瓶颈。

Conclusion: 潜空间的几何结构应作为物理接地视觉和世界模型的一等设计选择，对保留3D几何和物理一致性至关重要。

Abstract: Modern visual world modeling systems increasingly rely on high-capacity architectures and large-scale data to produce plausible motion, yet they often fail to preserve underlying 3D geometry or physically consistent camera dynamics. A key limitation lies not only in model capacity, but in the latent representations used to encode geometric structure. We propose S$^2$VAE, a geometry-first latent learning framework that focuses on compressing and representing the latent 3D state of a scene, including camera motion, depth, and point-level structure, rather than modeling appearance alone. Building on representations from a Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT), we introduce a novel type of variational autoencoder using a product of Power Spherical latent distributions, explicitly enforcing hyperspherical structure in the bottleneck to preserve directional and geometric semantics under strong compression. Across depth estimation, camera pose recovery, and point cloud reconstruction, we show that geometry-aligned hyperspherical latents consistently outperform conventional Gaussian bottlenecks, particularly in high-compression regimes. Our results highlight latent geometry as a first-class design choice for physically grounded visual and world models.

</details>


### [129] [AdvDMD: Adversarial Reward Meets DMD For High-Quality Few-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28126)
*Xu Wang,Zexian Li,Litong Gong,Tiezheng Ge,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdvDMD通过将DMD蒸馏与强化学习无缝统一，利用对抗训练判别器作为奖励模型，在少量采样步数下实现超越教师模型的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型蒸馏方法在采样步数有限时性能下降明显，而简单组合RL与蒸馏的方式引入了不必要的复杂性。

Method: AdvDMD将DMD2中的对抗训练判别器作为奖励模型，对生成图像给予低分、真实图像给予高分；在去噪过程的中间和最终状态上训练判别器，并与蒸馏模型在线更新；采用统一的SDE反向模拟以及不同的DMD和RL训练调度。

Result: 4步AdvDMD在DPG-Bench上超越原始40步SD3.5模型；在GenEval上对SD3取得显著性能提升；2步AdvDMD在Qwen-Image上超越TwinFlow。

Conclusion: AdvDMD通过统一DMD蒸馏与RL框架，有效提升了少步生成质量，甚至超越教师模型，验证了方法的有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer superior generation quality at the expense of extensive sampling steps.
  Distillation methods, with Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) as a popular example, can mitigate this issue, but performance degradation remains pronounced when sampling steps are limited.
  Reinforcement learning (RL) has been leveraged to improve the few-step generation quality during distillation, with the potential to even surpass the performance of the teacher model. However, existing approaches are combinatorial in nature, merely integrating an RL process with the distillation process, which introduces unnecessary complexities. To address this gap, we propose AdvDMD, a method that seamlessly unifies DMD distillation and RL.
  Specifically, AdvDMD employs the adversarially trained discriminator from DMD2 as the reward model, which assigns low scores to generated images and high scores to real ones.
  It is trained on both intermediate and final states of the denoising process and updated online with the distilled model, enabling a holistic supervision of the sampling trajectories and mitigating reward hacking.
  We adopt a unified SDE backward simulation and a different training schedule for DMD and RL to enable a more stable and efficient training.
  Experimental results demonstrate that the 4-step AdvDMD outperforms the original 40-step model for SD3.5 on DPG-Bench, while achieving significant performance gains for SD3 on the GenEval.
  On Qwen-Image, our 2-step AdvDMD achieves superior performance over TwinFlow.

</details>


### [130] [MoCapAnything V2: End-to-End Motion Capture for Arbitrary Skeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28130)
*Kehong Gong,Zhengyu Wen,Dao Thien Phong,Mingxi Xu,Weixia He,Qi Wang,Ning Zhang,Zhengyu Li,Guanli Hou,Dongze Lian,Xiaoyu He,Mingyuan Zhang,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个完全端到端的任意骨架动捕框架，通过引入参考姿态-旋转对解决姿态到旋转映射的歧义问题，结合全局-局部图引导多头注意力模块，将旋转误差从~17度降至~10度（未见骨架6.54度），推理速度提升~20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单目视频的任意骨架动捕方法采用分解式流程（Video-to-Pose + 解析逆运动学），但关节位置不能完全确定旋转（如骨轴扭转自由度模糊），且不可微的IK阶段阻碍系统适应噪声预测或针对最终动画目标进行优化。

Method: 1) 提出首个完全端到端框架，Video-to-Pose和Pose-to-Rotation均可学习并联合优化；2) 引入目标资产的参考姿态-旋转对作为锚点，定义旋转坐标系，将旋转预测转化为有约束的条件问题；3) 两阶段共享骨架感知的全局-局部图引导多头注意力（GL-GMHA）模块。

Result: 在Truebones Zoo和Objaverse上，旋转误差从~17度降至~10度，未见骨架达6.54度；推理速度比基于网格的流程快~20倍。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过引入参考姿态-旋转对可以解决姿态到旋转映射的歧义，实现端到端可学习的动捕框架，显著提升精度和效率，为任意骨架动捕提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent methods for arbitrary-skeleton motion capture from monocular video follow a factorized pipeline, where a Video-to-Pose network predicts joint positions and an analytical inverse-kinematics (IK) stage recovers joint rotations. While effective, this design is inherently limited, since joint positions do not fully determine rotations and leave degrees of freedom such as bone-axis twist ambiguous, and the non-differentiable IK stage prevents the system from adapting to noisy predictions or optimizing for the final animation objective. In this work, we present the first fully end-to-end framework in which both Video-to-Pose and Pose-to-Rotation are learnable and jointly optimized. We observe that the ambiguity in pose-to-rotation mapping arises from missing coordinate system information: the same joint positions can correspond to different rotations under different rest poses and local axis conventions. To resolve this, we introduce a reference pose-rotation pair from the target asset, which, together with the rest pose, not only anchors the mapping but also defines the underlying rotation coordinate system. This formulation turns rotation prediction into a well-constrained conditional problem and enables effective learning. In addition, our model predicts joint positions directly from video without relying on mesh intermediates, improving both robustness and efficiency. Both stages share a skeleton-aware Global-Local Graph-guided Multi-Head Attention (GL-GMHA) module for joint-level local reasoning and global coordination. Experiments on Truebones Zoo and Objaverse show that our method reduces rotation error from ~17 degrees to ~10 degrees, and to 6.54 degrees on unseen skeletons, while achieving ~20x faster inference than mesh-based pipelines. Project page: https://animotionlab.github.io/MoCapAnythingV2/

</details>


### [131] [3D-ReGen: A Unified 3D Geometry Regeneration Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28134)
*Geon Yeong Park,Roman Shapovalov,Rakesh Ranjan,Jong Chul Ye,Andrea Vedaldi,Thu Nguyen-Phuoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-ReGen，一种基于初始3D形状条件化的3D重生成器，通过VecSet条件机制和自监督预训练，在3D增强、重建和编辑等多个任务上实现了可控3D生成的最新性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D生成器多为一次性（one-shot）转换，将文本或图像转换为3D对象，但可控性有限，难以支持多样化的3D编辑和增强任务。

Method: 提出3D-ReGen，以初始3D形状为条件进行重生成；设计基于VecSet的条件机制，使重生成器能够更新或改进输入几何并保持一致的细粒度细节；通过自监督预任务和数据增强从现有3D数据集学习广泛适用的重生成先验，无需额外标注。

Result: 在3D增强、重建和编辑等多个任务上，3D-ReGen在几何一致性和细粒度质量方面均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 基于初始形状条件化的3D重生成范式可行且有效，VecSet条件机制和自监督学习策略使模型能够灵活支持多种下游任务，同时保持高质量的生成结果。

Abstract: We consider the problem of regenerating 3D objects from 2D images and initial 3D shapes. Most 3D generators operate in a one-shot fashion, converting text or images to a 3D object with limited controllability. We introduce instead 3D-ReGen, a 3D regenerator that is conditioned on an initial 3D shape. This conceptually simple formulation allows us to support numerous useful tasks, including 3D enhancement, reconstruction, and editing. 3D-ReGen uses a new conditioning mechanism based on VecSet, which allows the regenerator to update or improve the input geometry with consistent fine-grained details. 3D-ReGen learns a widely applicable regeneration prior from off-the-shelf 3D datasets via self-supervised pretext tasks and augmentations, without additional annotations. We evaluate both the geometric consistency and fine-grained quality of 3D-ReGen, achieving state-of-the-art performance in controllable 3D generation across several tasks.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond Pixel Fidelity: Minimizing Perceptual Distortion and Color Bias in Night Photography Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28136)
*Furkan Kınlı*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: pHVI-ISPNet是一个基于HVI色彩空间的RAW-to-RGB框架，通过RAW域特征处理、小波特征传播、样本动态损失系数和基于特征分布的损失项四项改进，在NTIRE 2025夜景摄影渲染挑战中实现了CIE2000色差和LPIPS指标的最优结果，兼顾了高保真度与感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜景摄影渲染（NPR）面临场景中黑暗区域与强点光源之间极端对比度的挑战。现有方法主要针对保真度指标设计，存在显著的感知差距，常损害视觉质量。

Method: 提出pHVI-ISPNet，一种基于HVI色彩空间的RAW-to-RGB框架。包含四项关键改进：1）RAW域特征处理；2）基于小波的特征传播以缓解高频细节损失；3）样本动态损失系数确保不同曝光级别的稳定学习；4）基于特征分布的损失项以维持严格的色彩恒常性。

Result: 在NTIRE 2025夜景摄影渲染挑战数据集上，方法在保持有竞争力保真度的同时，在CIE2000色差和LPIPS两项指标上均达到最新最优水平。

Conclusion: 验证了感知驱动设计在高质量夜景成像中的有效性，实现了保真度与感知质量的平衡。

Abstract: Night Photography Rendering (NPR) poses a significant challenge due to the extreme contrast between dark and illuminated areas in scenes, stemming from concurrent capture of severely dark regions alongside intense point light sources. Existing methods, which are mainly tailored for fidelity metrics, reveal considerable perceptual gaps and often detract from visual quality. We introduce pHVI-ISPNet, a novel RAW-to-RGB framework built on the robust HVI color space. Our network integrates four distinct key refinements: RAW-domain feature processing and Wavelet-based feature propagation to mitigate high-frequency detail loss; sample-based dynamic loss coefficients to ensure stable learning across varying exposure levels; and loss term based on feature distributions to maintain rigorous color constancy. Evaluations on the dataset introduced in the NTIRE 2025 challenge on NPR confirm our approach achieves competitive fidelity while establishing new state-of-the-art results in both CIE2000 color difference and LPIPS. This validates our perceptually-driven design for high-quality nighttime imaging.

</details>


### [133] [PhyCo: Learning Controllable Physical Priors for Generative Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28169)
*Sriram Narayanan,Ziyu Jiang,Srinivasa Narasimhan,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyCo通过引入连续、可解释、物理可控的条件控制，显著提升视频扩散模型的物理一致性，无需推理时使用模拟器或几何重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在视觉外观合成上表现出色，但物理一致性差：物体漂移、碰撞缺乏真实反弹、材质响应与真实属性不匹配。

Method: (i) 构建包含10万+真实感仿真视频的大规模数据集，系统变化摩擦、恢复系数、形变和力； (ii) 基于物理属性图条件，使用ControlNet对预训练扩散模型进行物理监督微调； (iii) VLM引导的奖励优化，通过微调视觉语言模型对生成视频进行物理查询并提供可微反馈。

Result: 在Physics-IQ基准上显著提升了物理真实感，人类评估确认对物理属性的控制更清晰、更忠实。

Conclusion: 提供了一条可扩展的路径，实现物理一致且可控的生成式视频模型，且能够泛化到合成训练环境之外。

Abstract: Modern video diffusion models excel at appearance synthesis but still struggle with physical consistency: objects drift, collisions lack realistic rebound, and material responses seldom match their underlying properties. We present PhyCo, a framework that introduces continuous, interpretable, and physically grounded control into video generation. Our approach integrates three key components: (i) a large-scale dataset of over 100K photorealistic simulation videos where friction, restitution, deformation, and force are systematically varied across diverse scenarios; (ii) physics-supervised fine-tuning of a pretrained diffusion model using a ControlNet conditioned on pixel-aligned physical property maps; and (iii) VLM-guided reward optimization, where a fine-tuned vision-language model evaluates generated videos with targeted physics queries and provides differentiable feedback. This combination enables a generative model to produce physically consistent and controllable outputs through variations in physical attributes-without any simulator or geometry reconstruction at inference. On the Physics-IQ benchmark, PhyCo significantly improves physical realism over strong baselines, and human studies confirm clearer and more faithful control over physical attributes. Our results demonstrate a scalable path toward physically consistent, controllable generative video models that generalize beyond synthetic training environments.

</details>


### [134] [AEGIS: A Holistic Benchmark for Evaluating Forensic Analysis of AI-Generated Academic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28177)
*Bo Zhang,Tzu-Yen Ma,Zichen Tang,Junpeng Ding,Zirui Wang,Yizhuo Zhao,Peilin Gao,Zijie Xi,Zixin Ding,Haiyang Sun,Haocheng Gao,Yuan Liu,Liangjia Wang,Yiling Huang,Yujie Wang,Yuyue Zhang,Ronghui Xi,Yuanze Li,Jiacheng Liu,Zhongjun Yang,Haihong E*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AEGIS是一个面向AI生成学术图像取证的综合基准测试，涵盖7大学科39个子类、25种生成模型模拟的4类伪造策略，评估了25个多模态大语言模型和9个专家模型，发现现有模型在学术图像取证中存在根本性局限（GPT-5.1整体性能仅48.80%，专家模型定位IoU仅30.09%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏针对学术图像领域的专门评估，无法有效衡量和推动AI生成学术图像取证技术的发展。

Method: 构建包含7大学科39个细分类别的学术图像数据集；使用25种生成模型模拟4类学术伪造策略；设计多维度评估框架同时检测、推理和定位能力。

Result: GPT-5.1整体性能仅48.80%；专家模型定位IoU仅30.09%；11种生成模型导致平均取证准确率低于50%；MLLMs在文本伪影识别上达84.74%，专家模型在二分类真伪检测上达79.54%。

Conclusion: AEGIS揭示了当前学术图像取证方法落后于生成技术进步的现实，不同模型家族间存在互补优势，亟需进一步发展。

Abstract: We introduce AEGIS, A holistic benchmark for Evaluating forensic analysis of AI-Generated academic ImageS. Compared to existing benchmarks, AEGIS features three key advances: (1) Domain-Specific Complexity: covering seven academic categories with 39 fine-grained subtypes, exposing intrinsic forensic difficulty, where even GPT-5.1 reaches 48.80% overall performance and expert models achieve only limited localization accuracy (IoU 30.09%); (2) Diverse Forgery Simulations: modeling four prevalent academic forgery strategies across 25 generative models, with 11 yielding average forensic accuracy below 50%, showing that forensics lag behind generative advances; and (3) Multi-Dimensional Forensic Evaluation: jointly assessing detection, reasoning, and localization, revealing complementary strengths between model families, with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) at 84.74% accuracy in textual artifact recognition and expert detectors peaking at 79.54% accuracy in binary authenticity detection. By evaluating 25 leading MLLMs, nine expert models, and one unified multimodal understanding and generation model, AEGIS serves as a diagnostic testbed exposing fundamental limitations in academic image forensics.

</details>


### [135] [Stop Holding Your Breath: CT-Informed Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Bronchoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28179)
*Andrea Dunn Beltran,Daniel Rho,Aarav Mehta,Xinqi Xiong,Raúl San José Estépar,Ron Alterovitz,Marc Niethammer,Roni Sengupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需屏气协议的支气管镜导航方法，利用患者特定的吸呼气CT扫描构建呼吸变形模型，并通过网格锚定高斯泼溅框架从内窥镜RGB视频中实时估计呼吸相位，实现连续变形感知重建。在RESPIRE仿真平台上验证，该方法达到1.22mm定位精度（<3mm临床阈值），训练速度提升20倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 呼吸运动导致气道变形5-20mm，造成CT与体内解剖结构的差异，限制支气管镜导航的定位精度。现有屏气协议难以复现且干扰临床工作流，亟需一种无需屏气、无外部传感器的连续变形感知导航方法。

Method: 利用术前吸呼气双相CT配准构建患者特异性呼吸变形空间，将呼吸运动降维为单标量呼吸相位；将该表示嵌入网格锚定高斯泼溅框架，通过轻量级估计器从内窥镜RGB视频直接推断呼吸相位，实现全呼吸周期的连续变形重建。

Result: 在RESPIRE物理仿真平台上，该方法实现几何保真重建，训练速度提升20倍以上，目标定位精度达1.22mm，优于未考虑呼吸变形的单CT基线方法，且满足3mm临床容忍阈值。

Conclusion: 本方法通过患者特异性呼吸建模消除了屏气协议的需求，在保证临床精度要求的同时显著提升了导航系统的实用性和工作流兼容性。

Abstract: Bronchoscopic navigation relies on registering endoscopic video to a preoperative CT scan, but respiratory motion deforms the airway by 5-20 mm, creating CT-to-body divergence that limits localization accuracy. In practice, this is mitigated through breath-hold protocols, which attempt to match the intraoperative anatomy to a static CT, but are difficult to reproduce and disrupt clinical workflow. We propose to eliminate the need for breath-hold protocols by leveraging patient-specific respiratory modeling. Paired inhale-exhale CT scans, already acquired for planning, implicitly define the patient-specific deformation space of the breathing airway. By registering these scans, we reduce respiratory motion to a single scalar breathing phase per frame, constraining all reconstructions to anatomically observed configurations. We embed this representation within a mesh-anchored Gaussian splatting framework, where a lightweight estimator infers breathing phase directly from endoscopic RGB, enabling continuous, deformation-aware reconstruction throughout the respiratory cycle without breath-holds or external sensing. To enable quantitative evaluation, we introduce RESPIRE, a physically grounded bronchoscopy simulation pipeline with per-frame ground truth for geometry, pose, breathing phase, and deformation. Experiments on RESPIRE show that our approach achieves geometrically faithful reconstruction, over 20x faster training, and 1.22 mm target localization accuracy (within the 3mm clinically relevant tolerances) outperforming unconstrained single-CT baselines. Please check out our website for additional visuals: https://asdunnbe.github.io/RESPIRE/

</details>


### [136] [Visual Generation in the New Era: An Evolution from Atomic Mapping to Agentic World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28185)
*Keming Wu,Zuhao Yang,Kaichen Zhang,Shizun Wang,Haowei Zhu,Sicong Leng,Zhongyu Yang,Qijie Wang,Sudong Wang,Ziting Wang,Zili Wang,Hui Zhang,Haonan Wang,Hang Zhou,Yifan Pu,Xingxuan Li,Fangneng Zhan,Bo Li,Lidong Bing,Yuxin Song,Ziwei Liu,Wenhu Chen,Jingdong Wang,Xinchao Wang,Xiaojuan Qi,Shijian Lu,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉生成领域应从外观合成转向智能视觉生成，构建了五级分类体系（原子生成→世界建模生成），分析了关键技术驱动因素，揭示了当前评估过度强调感知质量而忽视结构、时序和因果缺陷的问题，并提供了能力导向的发展路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成模型在照片级真实感、指令遵循和交互编辑方面取得重大进展，但在空间推理、持久状态、长程一致性和因果理解方面仍然存在困难。作者认为领域应超越外观合成，迈向基于结构、动力学、领域知识和因果关系的智能视觉生成。

Method: 提出了五级分类体系：原子生成、条件生成、上下文生成、智能体生成和世界建模生成；分析了流匹配、统一理解与生成模型、改进的视觉表征、后训练、奖励建模、数据整理、合成数据蒸馏和采样加速等关键技术驱动因素；结合基准评测、野外压力测试和专家约束案例研究进行评估。

Result: 揭示了当前评估体系过度强调感知质量，未能有效捕捉结构、时序和因果方面的失败模式。

Conclusion: 提供了一份以能力为中心的理解、评估和推进下一代智能视觉生成系统的路线图。

Abstract: Recent visual generation models have made major progress in photorealism, typography, instruction following, and interactive editing, yet they still struggle with spatial reasoning, persistent state, long-horizon consistency, and causal understanding. We argue that the field should move beyond appearance synthesis toward intelligent visual generation: plausible visuals grounded in structure, dynamics, domain knowledge, and causal relations. To frame this shift, we introduce a five-level taxonomy: Atomic Generation, Conditional Generation, In-Context Generation, Agentic Generation, and World-Modeling Generation, progressing from passive renderers to interactive, agentic, world-aware generators. We analyze key technical drivers, including flow matching, unified understanding-and-generation models, improved visual representations, post-training, reward modeling, data curation, synthetic data distillation, and sampling acceleration. We further show that current evaluations often overestimate progress by emphasizing perceptual quality while missing structural, temporal, and causal failures. By combining benchmark review, in-the-wild stress tests, and expert-constrained case studies, this roadmap offers a capability-centered lens for understanding, evaluating, and advancing the next generation of intelligent visual generation systems.

</details>


### [137] [Representation Fréchet Loss for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28190)
*Jiawei Yang,Zhengyang Geng,Xuan Ju,Yonglong Tian,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FD-loss，通过将FD估计的群体大小与梯度计算的批次大小解耦，成功将原本被认为不适合作为训练目标的弗雷歇距离(FD)用于优化生成模型。实验表明该方法能显著提升视觉质量，在ImageNet 256x256上单步生成器达到0.72 FID，并能将多步生成器转化为强单步生成器，同时发现FID可能错误排名视觉质量，提出多表征指标FDr^k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弗雷歇距离(FD)长期被认为不适合作为训练目标，本文旨在证明通过巧妙的设计可以将其有效优化，从而为生成模型提供更好的训练目标和评估指标。

Method: 将FD估计所需的大规模群体大小（如50k）与梯度计算所需的批次大小（如1024）解耦，称为FD-loss；在多种表征空间中对基础生成器进行后训练优化；引入多表征评估指标FDr^k来弥补单一Inception FID的不足。

Result: 1) 在Inception特征空间下，单步生成器在ImageNet 256x256上达到0.72 FID；2) 无需教师蒸馏、对抗训练或逐样本目标，即可将多步生成器转化为强单步生成器；3) 发现FID可能错误排名视觉质量，现代表征下更优的样本可能对应更差的Inception FID。

Conclusion: FD可以成为有效的训练目标，鼓励在多样化的表征空间中探索分布距离作为生成模型的训练目标和评估指标。

Abstract: We show that Fréchet Distance (FD), long considered impractical as a training objective, can in fact be effectively optimized in the representation space. Our idea is simple: decouple the population size for FD estimation (e.g., 50k) from the batch size for gradient computation (e.g., 1024). We term this approach FD-loss. Optimizing FD-loss reveals several surprising findings. First, post-training a base generator with FD-loss in different representation spaces consistently improves visual quality. Under the Inception feature space, a one-step generator achieves0.72 FID on ImageNet 256x256. Second, the same FD-loss repurposes multi-step generators into strong one-step generators without teacher distillation, adversarial training or per-sample targets. Third, FID can misrank visual quality: modern representations can yield better samples despite worse Inception FID. This motivates FDr$^k$, a multi-representation metric. We hope this work will encourage further exploration of distributional distances in diverse representation spaces as both training objectives and evaluation metrics for generative models.

</details>


### [138] [Generalizable Sparse-View 3D Reconstruction from Unconstrained Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28193)
*Vinayak Gupta,Chih-Hao Lin,Shenlong Wang,Anand Bhattad,Jia-Bin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenWildSplat是一个前馈式框架，无需场景特定优化即可从无位姿的稀疏互联网图像中重建3D场景，通过外观适配器和语义分割处理光照变化和瞬态遮挡，在多个基准上达到实时推理的最优渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖场景特定的外观嵌入或动态掩码优化，在稀疏视图下效果差且泛化能力不足；同时缺乏在多样化真实条件下的评估。

Method: 提出前馈框架GenWildSplat，从无位姿稀疏图像中预测深度、相机参数和规范空间中的3D高斯；外观适配器调节目标光照条件，语义分割处理瞬态物体；采用课程学习策略在合成和真实数据上训练。

Result: 在PhotoTourism和MegaScenes基准上达到前馈式方法的最优渲染质量，实现实时推理而无需测试时优化。

Conclusion: GenWildSplat有效解决了稀疏无位姿图像在真实条件下重建的挑战，具有良好的泛化能力和实时性能。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D scenes from sparse, unposed images remains challenging under real-world conditions with varying illumination and transient occlusions. Existing methods rely on scene-specific optimization using appearance embeddings or dynamic masks, which requires extensive per-scene training and fails under sparse views. Moreover, evaluations on limited scenes raise questions about generalization. We present GenWildSplat, a feed-forward framework for sparse-view outdoor reconstruction that requires no per-scene optimization. Given unposed internet images, GenWildSplat predicts depth, camera parameters, and 3D Gaussians in a canonical space using learned geometric priors. An appearance adapter modulates appearance for target lighting conditions, while semantic segmentation handles transient objects. Through curriculum learning on synthetic and real data, GenWildSplat generalizes across diverse illumination and occlusion patterns. Evaluations on PhotoTourism and MegaScenes benchmark demonstrate state-of-the-art feed-forward rendering quality, achieving real-time inference without test-time optimization

</details>


### [139] [HERMES++: Toward a Unified Driving World Model for 3D Scene Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28196)
*Xin Zhou,Dingkang Liang,Xiwu Chen,Feiyang Tan,Dingyuan Zhang,Hengshuang Zhao,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HERMES++是一个统一的驾驶世界模型，通过BEV表示、LLM增强的世界查询、当前到未来的时序链接和联合几何优化策略，同时实现了3D场景理解与未来几何预测，在多个基准上超越了现有专用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型主要关注未来场景生成，缺乏全面的3D场景理解；而大语言模型虽具备推理能力，却无法预测未来几何演化，导致语义解释与物理模拟之间存在显著差距。

Method: 1) BEV表示将多视图空间信息整合为与LLM兼容的结构；2) LLM增强的世界查询促进理解分支的知识迁移；3) 当前到未来的时序链接桥接时间间隙，以语义条件驱动几何演化；4) 联合几何优化策略整合显式几何约束与隐式潜在正则化，对齐内部表示与几何先验。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量评估验证了方法的有效性，HERMES++在未来点云预测和3D场景理解任务上均超越了专用方法。

Conclusion: HERMES++通过统一框架有效弥合了语义理解与几何预测之间的鸿沟，实现了强性能，代码和模型将开源。

Abstract: Driving world models serve as a pivotal technology for autonomous driving by simulating environmental dynamics. However, existing approaches predominantly focus on future scene generation, often overlooking comprehensive 3D scene understanding. Conversely, while Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive reasoning capabilities, they lack the capacity to predict future geometric evolution, creating a significant disparity between semantic interpretation and physical simulation. To bridge this gap, we propose HERMES++, a unified driving world model that integrates 3D scene understanding and future geometry prediction within a single framework. Our approach addresses the distinct requirements of these tasks through synergistic designs. First, a BEV representation consolidates multi-view spatial information into a structure compatible with LLMs. Second, we introduce LLM-enhanced world queries to facilitate knowledge transfer from the understanding branch. Third, a Current-to-Future Link is designed to bridge the temporal gap, conditioning geometric evolution on semantic context. Finally, to enforce structural integrity, we employ a Joint Geometric Optimization strategy that integrates explicit geometric constraints with implicit latent regularization to align internal representations with geometry-aware priors. Extensive evaluations on multiple benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our method. HERMES++ achieves strong performance, outperforming specialist approaches in both future point cloud prediction and 3D scene understanding tasks. The model and code will be publicly released at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/HERMESV2.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [C8s: A Confidential Kubernetes Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26974)
*Amean Asad,Patrick McClurg,João Andrade*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出C8s架构，基于AMD SEV-SNP、Intel TDX和NVIDIA机密计算等硬件TEE，为Kubernetes集群提供密码学可验证的机密性、完整性和可证明性保障，兼容EKS、GKE、AKS等托管Kubernetes服务，面向数据/工件所有者、计算提供商和终端用户三类角色，适用于AI推理、模型权重保护和敏感数据训练等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kubernetes部署中，基础设施运营商（如云服务商）可访问租户的工作负载和数据，缺乏密码学级别的机密性与完整性保证。托管Kubernetes服务的控制平面无法被可信执行环境（TEE）覆盖，导致安全缺口。

Method: 基于硬件TEE（AMD SEV-SNP、Intel TDX、NVIDIA机密计算）构建以远程证明（Attestation）为根的可信边界，将机密虚拟机（Confidential VMs）纳入保护范围，兼容无法证明的托管控制平面，并设计密码学可验证的安全保障机制。

Result: 提出了C8s架构，实现了三类角色的新安全保障：数据与工件所有者可在第三方基础设施上安全部署敏感工作负载；计算提供商可在不向云运营商暴露工作负载的情况下提供执行服务；终端用户的请求除经证明的TEE外，对任何方保持不透明。

Conclusion: C8s为Kubernetes集群提供了密码学根植的机密性、完整性和可证明性，使其可适用于AI推理、模型权重保护、敏感数据训练/微调等安全敏感场景。

Abstract: This paper presents C8s, a confidential computing architecture for Kubernetes that provides cryptographically rooted confidentiality, integrity, and verifiability guarantees for Kubernetes clusters from infrastructure operators. These guarantees are cryptographically provable to any independent third party verifier. The architecture is built on hardware Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), specifically AMD SEV-SNP, Intel TDX, and NVIDIA Confidential Computing support, to establish an attestation-rooted trust boundary around confidential VMs. This design is compatible with managed Kubernetes services such as Amazon EKS, Google GKE, and Microsoft AKS, where the control plane cannot be attested. Under this boundary, three groups gain guarantees that are absent from conventional deployments. Data and artifact owners can deploy sensitive workloads and proprietary artifacts on third-party infrastructure without risking exfiltration. Compute providers can offer execution services without revealing workloads to cloud operators. End users can submit requests that remain opaque to all parties except the attested TEE processing them. Representative workloads include AI inference, securing AI model weights, and training or fine-tuning on sensitive data.

</details>


### [141] [An Empirical Security Evaluation of LLM-Generated Cryptographic Rust Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27001)
*Mohamed Elsayed,Kenneth Fulton,Jeong Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对3个LLM生成的240个Rust加密代码样本进行评估，发现仅23.3%编译成功，57%存在漏洞，通用分析工具不足，且提示策略显著影响结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者和组织越来越多地使用LLM生成安全关键代码（包括加密方案），但缺乏对其生成代码安全性的系统评估，需要实证研究来揭示潜在风险。

Method: 收集3个LLM（Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT-4o、DeepSeek Coder）在4种提示策略下生成的240个AES-256-GCM和ChaCha20-Poly1305 Rust代码样本，对编译成功的样本使用CodeQL静态分析和基于规则的加密专用分析器检测漏洞，并与CWE关联。

Result: 仅23.3%样本编译成功；CodeQL仅产生2个假阳性，而加密专用分析器在编译样本中发现57%存在漏洞且零假阳性；AES-256-GCM编译成功率（34.2%）显著高于ChaCha20-Poly1305（12.5%）；模型选择无显著影响，但提示策略显著影响编译成功率（P=0.002），链式思维提示比零样本差5倍；所有模型存在系统性失败（如nonce重用和API幻觉）。

Conclusion: 通用分析工具不足以验证加密代码安全性，需专用分析工具；LLM生成加密代码的可靠性和安全性仍存在显著问题，需改进提示策略和模型能力。

Abstract: Developers and organizations are using Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate security-critical code more frequently than ever, including cryptographic solutions for their products. This study presents an empirical evaluation of cryptographic security in 240 Rust code samples for two crypto algorithms (AES-256-GCM and ChaCha20-Poly1305) generated by three LLMs (Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek Coder) using four different prompt strategies. For each successfully compiled code sample, CodeQL static analysis and our rule-based crypto-specific analyzer were used to detect vulnerabilities, which are also associated with Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE). The evaluation results revealed that only 23.3% of the generated code samples were successfully compiled. Among the compiled code, CodeQL produced only two false positives, while our rule-based crypto-specific analyzer identified vulnerabilities in 57% of the compiled samples with zero false positives. This demonstrates that general-purpose analysis tools are insufficient for code validation for the experimented crypto algorithms. The compilation success of the two algorithms varied significantly (AES-256-GCM 34.2% versus ChaCha20-Poly1305 12.5%), showing a gap in code generation capabilities. While model choice had no significant effect on compilation success, prompt strategy significantly influenced outcomes (P = 0.002), with chain-of-thought prompting performing 5 times worse than zero-shot. All three models exhibit systematic failures, including nonce reuse and API hallucinations.

</details>


### [142] [Membership Inference Attacks Against Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27002)
*Wei Song,Yuxin Cao,Ziqi Ding,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Yuekang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次探索视频大语言模型（VideoLLMs）的黑盒成员推理攻击，通过温度扰动生成与视频难度特征结合的方法，在LLaVA-Video-7B模型上实现了0.68 AUC和0.63准确率，揭示了视频大语言模型存在隐私泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型在异构视频-文本语料上训练或指令微调，引发隐私问题：外部审计者能否判断特定视频是否被用于训练？目前成员推理攻击在分类器、文本和图像生成模型上已有研究，但在VideoLLM领域尚属空白。

Method: 采用黑盒攻击方式，联合温度扰动生成与视频感知难度特征。在低温和高温下查询目标模型，测量生成文本之间的语义漂移，并结合视频内在难度特征（如运动复杂度、时间跨度）进行成员推断。

Result: 针对LLaVA-Video-7B-Qwen2-Video-Only模型，成员推理AUC达到0.68，准确率为0.63。

Conclusion: 视频大语言模型易受黑盒成员推理攻击，表明该领域亟需系统评估和缓解隐私风险。

Abstract: Video large language models (VideoLLMs) are increasingly trained or instruction-tuned on large-scale video--text corpora collected from heterogeneous sources, raising an immediate privacy question: can an external auditor determine whether a particular video was used during training? While membership inference attacks (MIAs) have been studied extensively for classifiers and, more recently, for text and image generation models, the VideoLLM setting remains unexplored. This setting is challenging because black-box auditors observe only generated text, whereas the membership signal is entangled with video-specific factors such as motion complexity and temporal span. In this paper, we present a black-box MIA targeting VideoLLMs that couples temperature-perturbed generation with video-aware difficulty features. Our key intuition is that member samples tend to induce sharper, more brittle generation behavior across decoding temperatures, and that this signal should be interpreted jointly with the intrinsic difficulty of the queried video. Concretely, we query the target model at low and high temperatures, measure the semantic drift between the resulting texts. We evaluate the attack against \texttt{LLaVA-Video-7B-Qwen2-Video-Only} and achieve a member inference AUC of 0.68 and accuracy of 0.63. These results demonstrate that Video-LLMs are vulnerable to black-box membership inference attacks, highlighting an urgent need for the community to systematically evaluate and mitigate privacy risks in VideoLLMs.

</details>


### [143] [Enhancing Linux Privilege Escalation Attack Capabilities of Local LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27143)
*Benjamin Probst,Andreas Happe,Jürgen Cito*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了能否通过针对性系统级和提示干预手段，弥合开源轻量级大模型与云端模型在自动化Linux权限提升任务上的性能差距。实验表明，通过链式思考、检索增强生成、结构化提示、历史压缩和反思分析等五种干预措施，Llama3.1 70B等开源模型可达到甚至超越GPT-4o等云端基线，其中反思类干预贡献最大，漏洞发现仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端大模型用于自动化渗透测试存在安全、隐私和主权问题，而此前研究表明小规模开源模型在Linux权限提升任务上表现不佳，限制了实际应用。作者希望探究能否通过系统性干预弥补这一性能差距。

Method: 通过系统实证研究，先分析开源模型在自主权限提升中的失败模式，再将其映射到已知增强技术，最终在hackingBuddyGPT框架上实现了五种具体干预措施（链式思考提示、检索增强生成、结构化提示、历史压缩和反思分析），并进行全因子消融实验评估各干预组合的效果。

Result: 启用干预后，Llama3.1 70B成功利用83%的测试漏洞；小模型如Llama3.1 8B和Qwen2.5 7B在引导下达到67%。全因子消融实验表明反思类干预贡献最大，同时发现漏洞发现是本地模型的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 开源轻量级模型通过适当的系统级和提示干预，可以在自动化权限提升任务上达到甚至超越云端模型的水平，但漏洞发现能力仍需进一步提升。

Abstract: Recent research has demonstrated the potential of Large Language Models (LLMs) for autonomous penetration testing, particularly when using cloud-based restricted-weight models. However, reliance on such models introduces security, privacy, and sovereignty concerns, motivating the use of locally hosted open-weight alternatives. Prior work shows that small open-weight models perform poorly on automated Linux privilege escalation, limiting their practical applicability.
  In this paper, we present a systematic empirical study of whether targeted system-level and prompting interventions can bridge this performance gap. We analyze failure modes of open-weight models in autonomous privilege escalation, map them to established enhancement techniques, and evaluate five concrete interventions (chain-of-thought prompting, retrieval-augmented generation, structured prompts, history compression, and reflective analysis) implemented as extensions to hackingBuddyGPT.
  Our results show that open-weight models can match or outperform cloud-based baselines such as GPT-4o. With our treatments enabled, Llama3.1 70B exploits 83% of tested vulnerabilities, while smaller models including Llama3.1 8B and Qwen2.5 7B achieve 67% when using guidance. A full-factorial ablation study over all treatment combinations reveals that reflection-based treatments contribute most, while also identifying vulnerability discovery as a remaining bottleneck for local models.

</details>


### [144] [REBENCH: A Procedural, Fair-by-Construction Benchmark for LLMs on Stripped-Binary Types and Names (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27319)
*Jun Yeon Won,Xin Jin,Shiqing Ma,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出REBench，一个用于评估LLM在二进制逆向工程任务中表现的标准基准数据集，解决了现有研究因缺乏统一数据集而难以公平比较的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在二进制逆向工程（如函数/变量名恢复、类型推断）中的应用研究增长迅速，但缺乏标准化数据集。现有研究使用不同的数据集、预处理流程和评估指标，导致不同方法之间难以进行公平比较，阻碍了对LLM在二进制分析中能力的清晰理解。

Method: REBench整合了现有数据集的超集，包含数亿行源代码和跨多种架构及优化级别的多样化二进制文件。采用基于知识库的方法，存储字节级栈信息以生成ground truth，在保留任务难度的同时确保通用适用性，避免简化导致的偏差。

Result: 将REBench应用于评估LLM的逆向工程性能，结果表明在复杂任务中存在困难。

Conclusion: REBench能够支持对LLM在逆向工程任务上的公平评估，揭示了当前LLM在处理复杂逆向工程任务时的局限性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in recent years, driving their adoption across a wide range of domains, including computer security. In reverse engineering, LLMs are increasingly applied to critical tasks such as function and variable name recovery and type inference. However, despite the rapid growth of research in this area, progress has been hindered by the absence of a standardized dataset. Existing studies rely on disparate datasets, preprocessing pipelines, and evaluation metrics, making fair comparisons between approaches difficult and obscuring a clear understanding of LLM capabilities in binary analysis. To address these challenges, we present REBench, a comprehensive benchmark dataset for evaluating LLMs on binary reverse engineering tasks. REBench consolidates a superset of existing datasets, comprising hundreds of millions of lines of source code and a diverse collection of binaries spanning multiple architectures and optimization levels. REBench adopts a knowledge-base-driven methodology that stores byte-level stack information to generate ground truth, ensuring that task difficulty is preserved while maintaining universal applicability. This design enables fair evaluation across tasks while avoiding simplifications that could bias results. As a use case, we apply REBench to measure the reverse engineering performance of LLMs and the result demonstrates difficulties in complex tasks.

</details>


### [145] [Toward Autonomous SOC Operations: End-to-End LLM Framework for Threat Detection, Query Generation, and Resolution in Security Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27321)
*Md Hasan Saju,Akramul Azim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端威胁管理框架，集成集成检测、语法约束查询生成（SQM架构）和检索增强解决支持，在SOC环境中将平均事件分类时间从数小时缩短至10分钟以内，检测准确率达82.8%，解决方案代码预测准确率从78.3%提升至90.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC面临威胁数量增加、异构SIEM平台以及耗时的手动分类工作流程带来的运营挑战，亟需自动化安全关键工作流的解决方案。

Method: 提出端到端威胁管理框架：(1) 检测模块集成三种最佳LLM构建集成模型；(2) SQM架构利用平台特定语法约束、元数据检索和文档提示自动生成可执行查询；(3) 基于SQM证据的检索增强解决支持提升推荐生成质量。

Result: 集成检测模型准确率82.8%，假阳性率0.120；SQM在IBM QRadar和Google SecOps上BLEU 0.384、ROUGE-L 0.731，优于基线LLM两倍以上；加入SQM证据后解决方案代码预测准确率从78.3%提升至90.0%，推荐质量评分8.70；生产环境中平均事件分类时间从数小时降至10分钟以内。

Conclusion: 领域约束的LLM架构结合检索增强能够在运营安全环境中满足严格的可靠性和效率要求，实现规模化自动化。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) face mounting operational challenges. These challenges come from increasing threat volumes, heterogeneous SIEM platforms, and time-consuming manual triage workflows. We present an end-to-end threat management framework that integrates ensemble-based detection, syntax-constrained query generation, and retrieval-augmented resolution support to automate critical security workflows. Our detection module evaluates both traditional machine learning classifiers and large language models (LLMs), then combines the three best-performing LLMs to create an ensemble model, achieving 82.8% accuracy while maintaining 0.120 false positive rate on SIEM logs. We introduce the SQM (Syntax Query Metadata) architecture for automated evidence collection. It uses platform-specific syntax constraints, metadata-based retrieval, and documentation-grounded prompting to generate executable queries for IBM QRadar and Google SecOps. SQM achieves a BLEU score of 0.384 and a ROUGE-L score of 0.731. These results are more than twice as good as the baseline LLM performance. For incident resolution and recommendation generation, we demonstrate that integrating SQM-derived evidence improves resolution code prediction accuracy from 78.3% to 90.0%, with an overall recommendation quality score of 8.70. In production SOC environments, our framework reduces average incident triage time from hours to under 10 minutes. This work demonstrates that domain-constrained LLM architectures with retrieval augmentation can meet the strict reliability and efficiency requirements of operational security environments at scale.

</details>


### [146] [Secret Stealing Attacks on Local LLM Fine-Tuning through Supply-Chain Model Code Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27426)
*Zi Li,Tian Zhou,Wenze Li,Jingyu Hua,Yunlong Mao,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种新型攻击向量：通过伪装成标准架构定义的恶意模型代码，在本地微调过程中实现确定性秘密窃取。该方法利用在线张量规则匹配和价值梯度解耦技术，克服梯度淹没问题，强制模型记忆API密钥等敏感令牌，并通过黑盒查询实现攻击者可验证的秘密提取，在保持主任务性能的同时达到98%以上的严格攻击成功率，并能绕过DP-SGD、语义审计和代码审计等防御措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动预训练权重投毒攻击对自然语言有效，但无法捕获稀疏高熵目标（如API密钥），因为其依赖概率性语义前缀。同时，本地微调被视为隐私边界，但恶意模型代码足以窃取训练数据中的敏感秘密。

Method: 1. 利用伪装成标准架构定义的模型代码作为供应链攻击向量，实现从被动权重投毒到主动执行劫持的范式转变；2. 提出确定性全链记忆机制：通过在线张量规则匹配锁定动态计算流中的令牌级秘密，利用价值梯度解耦技术克服梯度淹没，强制模型记忆；3. 首次实现基于黑盒查询的攻击者可验证秘密窃取，精确区分真实泄露与幻觉。

Result: 严格攻击成功率（Strict ASR）超过98%，且不损害主任务性能；能有效绕过DP-SGD、语义审计和代码审计等防御措施。

Conclusion: 本地微调并非安全的隐私边界，恶意模型代码可构成严重威胁；现有防御措施不足以防范此类攻击；需引起社区对模型供应链安全的高度重视。

Abstract: Local fine-tuning datasets routinely contain sensitive secrets such as API keys, personal identifiers, and financial records. Although ''local offline fine-tuning'' is often viewed as a privacy boundary, we reveal that compromised model code is sufficient to steal them. Current passive pretrained-weight poisoning attacks, while effective for natural language, fundamentally fail to capture such sparse high-entropy targets due to their reliance on probabilistic semantic prefixes. To bridge this gap, we identify and exploit a practical but overlooked supply-chain vector -- model code camouflaged as standard architectural definitions -- to realize a paradigm shift from passive weight poisoning to active execution hijacking. We introduce a deterministic full-chain memorization mechanism: it locks onto token-level secrets in dynamic computation flows via online tensor-rule matching, and leverages value-gradient decoupling to stealthily inject attack gradients, overcoming gradient drowning to force model memorization. Furthermore, we achieve, for the first time, attacker-verifiable secret stealing through black-box queries that precisely distinguishes true leakage from hallucination. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves over 98\% Strict ASR without compromising the primary task, and can effectively bypass defense measures including DP-SGD, semantic auditing, and code auditing.

</details>


### [147] [Secure Cross-Silo Synthetic Genomic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27456)
*Daniil Filienko,Martine De Cock,Sikha Pentyala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合安全多方计算（MPC）和差分隐私（DP）的方法，允许多个数据持有方在不泄露原始数据的情况下联合训练合成数据生成器，从而在保护隐私的同时实现基因组数据的广泛共享，并用于AI方法开发。实验在多个真实RNA-seq队列的联邦设置下验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组数据访问受到严格监管，繁琐的数据访问流程阻碍了AI方法在基因组学中的发展。合成数据生成虽可缓解这一问题，但许多应用场景（如罕见病研究）中，单一机构数据量不足以训练生成模型，需要跨机构联合数据，而直接共享原始数据存在隐私风险。

Method: 采用安全多方计算（MPC）确保输入隐私，使各方无需以未加密形式披露数据；结合差分隐私（DP）提供输出隐私，减轻合成数据发布中的信息泄露风险。多个数据持有方通过MPC在不暴露原始数据的情况下协同训练生成模型，再从中采样合成数据。

Result: 在多个真实RNA-seq队列的联邦设置下，该方法能够生成高质量的合成数据集，验证了在数据分布在不同机构时实现隐私保护数据合成的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为跨机构隐私保护的基因组数据合成提供了一种有效方案，在保障隐私的前提下促进了数据共享与AI方法开发。

Abstract: Access to genomic data is highly regulated due to its sensitive nature. While safeguards are essential, cumbersome data access processes pose a significant barrier to the development of AI methods for genomics. Synthetic data generation can mitigate this tension by enabling broader data sharing without exposing sensitive information. Synthetic genomic data are produced by training generative models on real data and subsequently sampling artificial data that preserves relevant statistics while limiting disclosures about the underlying individuals. In some settings, a single data holder may have sufficient data to train such generative models; however, in many applications data must be combined across multiple sites to achieve adequate scale. This need arises, e.g., in rare disease studies, where individual hospitals typically hold data for only a small number of patients. The solution we present in this paper enables multiple data holders to jointly train a synthetic data generator without revealing their raw data. Our approach combines secure multiparty computation (MPC) to ensure input privacy, so that no party ever discloses its data in unencrypted form, with differential privacy (DP) to provide output privacy by mitigating information leakage from the released synthetic data. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method by generating high-utility synthetic datasets from multiple real RNA-seq cohorts in federated settings, showing that our approach enables privacy-preserving data synthesis even when data are distributed across institutions.

</details>


### [148] [Security Attack and Defense Strategies for Autonomous Agent Frameworks: A Layered Review with OpenClaw as a Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27464)
*Luyao Xu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对基于大语言模型的自主智能体框架进行了分层安全综述，按上下文与指令层、工具与动作层、状态与持久层、生态与自动化层四个层面梳理了安全风险与防御策略，并以OpenClaw为案例进行分析，揭示了跨层威胁传播机制，指出了研究不平衡、长程评估缺失和生态信任模型薄弱等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体框架正发展为复杂、工具集成且持续运行的系统，引入了超越传统提示级漏洞的安全风险。该领域尚处早期，现有研究分散于各个安全方面，缺乏系统性的分层综述，因此迫切需要构建一个结构化的安全分析框架。

Method: 采用分层分析方法，将自主智能体框架的安全问题划分为四个层次（上下文与指令层、工具与动作层、状态与持久层、生态与自动化层），每层总结了功能角色、代表性安全风险和对应防御策略，并以OpenClaw作为案例进行具体分析，同时考察了威胁的跨层传播路径。

Result: 识别出威胁可从经过操纵的输入传播到不安全动作、持久状态污染及更广泛的生态系统影响；发现各层面间研究不平衡、缺乏长程安全评估、生态信任模型薄弱等关键问题。

Conclusion: 需要建立更系统和集成的防御方案，未来的研究方向包括平衡各层安全关注度、发展长程安全评估方法以及强化生态系统层面的信任模型。

Abstract: Autonomous agent frameworks built upon large language models (LLMs) are evolving into complex, tool-integrated, and continuously operating systems, introducing security risks beyond traditional prompt-level vulnerabilities. As this paradigm is still at an early stage of development, a timely and systematic understanding of its security implications is increasingly important. Although a growing body of work has examined different attack surfaces and defense problems in agent systems, existing studies remain scattered across individual aspects of agent security, and there is still a lack of a layered review on this topic. To address this gap, this survey presents a layered review of security risks and defense strategies in autonomous agent frameworks, with OpenClaw as a case study. We organize the analysis into four security-relevant layers: the context and instruction layer, the tool and action layer, the state and persistence layer, and the ecosystem and automation layer. For each layer, we summarize its functional role, representative security risks, and corresponding defense strategies. Based on this layered analysis, we further identify that threats in autonomous agent frameworks may propagate across layers, from manipulated inputs to unsafe actions, persistent state contamination, and broader ecosystem-level impact. Finally, we highlight potential key challenges, including research imbalance across layers, the lack of long-horizon evaluation, and weak ecosystem trust models, and outline future directions toward more systematic and integrated defenses.

</details>


### [149] [SecGoal: A Benchmark for Security Goal Extraction and Formalization from Protocol Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27601)
*Dawei Huang,Hui Li,Haonan Feng,Jingjing Guan,Yueshuang Jiao,Bo Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SecGoal——首个专家标注的协议安全目标基准数据集（覆盖15份真实协议文档，含5G-AKA和TLS 1.3），以及AIFG——一个将安全目标提取与形式化分解为两阶段的AI辅助框架。实验发现前沿大模型召回率高但精确率低于15%，而基于SecGoal微调的7B/9B小模型F1超过80%，为自动化形式化协议分析建立了基础数据集和可复现基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化地从自然语言协议文档中提取并形式化安全目标是形式化验证的关键瓶颈，因为缺乏专家标注资源和连接非结构化文本与符号逻辑的集成框架。

Method: 构建SecGoal专家标注基准（15份协议文档），提出AIFG框架，将任务分解为上下文感知的目标提取和检索增强的形式化两个阶段，并用LLM进行综合评估。

Result: 前沿模型（如Gemini 2.5-Pro）召回率高但精确率低于15%；基于SecGoal微调的7B/9B模型F1超过80%，显著优于更大的通用模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为自动化形式化协议分析提供了基础数据集和可复现基线，证明在小模型上进行指令微调比大通用模型更适合该任务。

Abstract: Formal verification provides rigorous guarantees for cryptographic security, yet automating the extraction and formalization of security goals from natural language protocol documents remains a major bottleneck, compounded by the scarcity of expert-annotated resources and integrated frameworks bridging unstructured text and symbolic logic. We introduce SecGoal, the first expert-annotated benchmark covering 15 widely deployed protocol documents, including 5G-AKA and TLS 1.3, and AIFG, an AI-assisted framework that decomposes the task into context-aware goal extraction and retrieval-augmented formalization. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation to assess whether contemporary LLMs are ready to automate this pipeline. Our results reveal a pronounced precision-recall imbalance: frontier models, such as Gemini 2.5-Pro, achieve high recall but precision below 15%, frequently misclassifying operational text as security goals. In contrast, instruction tuning on SecGoal enables compact models with 7B/9B parameters to achieve F1-scores above 80%, substantially outperforming larger general-purpose models. Our work establishes a foundational dataset and reproducible baseline for automated formal protocol analysis.

</details>


### [150] [VOW: Verifiable and Oblivious Watermark Detection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27666)
*Xiaokun Luan,Yihao Zhang,Pengcheng Su,Feiran Lei,Meng Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出VOW协议，通过VOPRF实现保护隐私且可加密验证的LLM水印检测，适用于短文本，并重新评估了水印对 paraphrasing 攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方法依赖中心化信任模型，用户需向服务商暴露敏感文本且无法验证结果完整性；现有非对称方案不适用于短文本或缺乏正式安全保证。

Method: 将水印检测形式化为安全两方计算问题，使用可验证不经意伪随机函数（VOPRF）实例化水印核心逻辑，实现用户文本不泄露、服务商结果可验证的检测协议。

Result: VOW协议在短文本场景下具有实用性，并提供了对水印在 paraphrasing 攻击下鲁棒性的重新评估。

Conclusion: VOW实现了兼具隐私保护和可加密验证的高效水印检测，推动了LLM水印的去中心化信任范式。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) watermarking is crucial for establishing the provenance of machine-generated text, but most existing methods rely on a centralized trust model. This model forces users to reveal potentially sensitive text to a provider for detection and offers no way to verify the integrity of the result. While asymmetric schemes have been proposed to address these issues, they are either impractical for short texts or lack formal guarantees linking watermark insertion and detection. We propose VOW, a new protocol that achieves both privacy-preserving and cryptographically verifiable watermark detection with high efficiency. Our approach formulates detection as a secure two-party computation problem, instantiating the watermark's core logic with a Verifiable Oblivious Pseudorandom Function (VOPRF). This allows the user and provider to perform detection without the user's text being revealed, while the provider's result is verifiable. Our comprehensive evaluation shows that VOW is practical for short texts and provides a crucial reassessment of watermark robustness against modern paraphrasing attacks.

</details>


### [151] [How Code Representation Shapes False-Positive Dynamics in Cross-Language LLM Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27714)
*Maofei Chen,Laifu Wang,Yue Qin,Yuan Wang,Bo Wu,Dongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了代码表示格式（原始文本 vs. 剪枝抽象语法树）对跨语言大模型漏洞检测中假阳性率（FPR）的影响。通过对两个8B参数模型（Qwen3-8B和Llama 3.1-8B）在C/C++数据上微调，并在Java和Python上评估，发现文本微调通过记忆表面线索（C/C++特有API名称和语法习惯）导致FPR单调上升，而AST探针可显著降低FPR。直接AST微调因平坦线性化引入新的结构表面线索而失效。提出了一种无需重训练的预部署一致性门控机制作为过滤假阳性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对代码表示格式如何影响跨语言LLM漏洞检测的假阳性行为理解不足，特别是训练时和推理时表示格式的联合效应尚不清晰。

Method: 系统性地变化训练强度和代码表示格式（原始文本 vs. 剪枝AST），在训练和推理时分别比较。使用两个8B参数LLM（Qwen3-8B和Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct），在NIST Juliet Test Suite的C/C++数据上微调，在Java（OWASP Benchmark）和Python（BenchmarkPython）上评估。设计了跨表示探针实验（文本训练权重直接应用于AST编码输入）来隔离表面线索记忆机制。

Result: 文本微调导致FPR单调上升（Qwen3-8B: 0.763零样本→0.866试点→1.000全量），而F1保持稳定（0.637-0.688），掩盖了模型性能的崩溃。跨表示探针使Qwen3-8B的FPR从0.866降至0.583，37.2%的假阳性在AST输入下恢复为真阴性。直接AST微调无法保持优势（FPR≥0.970）。AST探针在Python上FPR=0.554，与Java结果仅差2.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 文本微调通过表面线索记忆导致跨语言假阳性激增，AST表示可有效缓解但直接AST微调会引入新的表面线索。提出预部署一致性门控机制（同时通过文本和AST路径运行告警）作为无需重训练的假阳性过滤方案，但以牺牲召回率为代价。

Abstract: How code representation format shapes false positive behaviour in cross-language LLM vulnerability detection remains poorly understood. We systematically vary training intensity and code representation format, comparing raw source text with pruned Abstract Syntax Trees at both training time and inference time, across two 8B-parameter LLMs (Qwen3-8B and Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct) fine-tuned on C/C++ data from the NIST Juliet Test Suite (v1.3) and evaluated on Java (OWASP Benchmark v1.2) and Python (BenchmarkPython v0.1).
  Cross-language FPR reflects the joint effect of training-time and inference-time representation, not either alone. Text fine-tuning drives FPR upward monotonically (Qwen3-8B: 0.763 zero-shot, 0.866 pilot, 1.000 full-scale) while F1 remains stable (0.637-0.688), masking the collapse. We argue surface-cue memorisation is the primary mechanism: text fine-tuning encodes C/C++-specific API names and syntactic idioms as vulnerability triggers that fire indiscriminately on target-language code. A cross-representation probe, applying text-trained weights to AST-encoded input without retraining, isolates this: Qwen3-8B FPR drops from 0.866 to 0.583, and 37.2% of false positives revert to true negatives under AST input alone. Direct AST fine-tuning does not preserve the benefit (FPR at least 0.970), as flat linearisation introduces structural surface cues of its own. The pattern replicates across both model families. On BenchmarkPython the AST probe yields FPR=0.554, within 2.9 percentage points of the Java result, despite maximal surface-syntax differences, substantially weakening a domain-shift explanation. These findings motivate a pre-deployment consistency gate, running alerts through both text and AST paths, as a retraining-free filter for false-positive-sensitive settings, at the cost of reduced recall.

</details>


### [152] [Variational and Majorization Principles in Lattice Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27801)
*Javier Blanco-Romero,Florina Almenares Mendoza*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文利用majorization理论描述格基约化中Lovász交换的平滑机制，证明每个非退化Lovász交换都是对日志范数剖面的T-变换，从而任何严格Schur-凸的散布度量都会在该交换下减小。基于此，推导出最坏情况GSA包络的变分解释（唯一最小方差剖面）以及方差耗散的精确 telescoping 恒等式。进一步提出热力学族Schur-凸评分规则，并给出两个具体选择器：Thermal-Adaptive（在平坦剖面上减少操作次数）和Geodesic Deep-LLL（在结构格上减少等效交换次数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示格基约化中Lovász交换平滑Gram-Schmidt剖面的底层机制，从理论上解释为什么LLL算法能有效降低剖面散布，并为设计更高效的启发式算法提供理论基础。

Method: 使用majorization理论建模Lovász交换，证明其等价于日志范数剖面上的T-变换；利用Schur-凸性分析剖面散布的单调递减性；通过变分法推导最坏情况GSA包络；提出热力学族评分规则并通过自适应选择实现两个具体算法。

Result: 1) 最坏情况GSA包络具有变分解释，是唯一满足Lovász间隙几何的最小方差剖面；2) 方差耗散满足telescoping恒等式；3) Thermal-Adaptive在平坦剖面上比SS-GG减少操作次数，在q-ary输入上恢复SS-GG性能；4) Geodesic Deep-LLL在结构格上减少等效交换次数但墙钟时间更高。

Conclusion: Majorization视角统一了LLL算法的理论分析和启发式改进，既提供了严格的理论保证（GSA包络的最优性），又指导了实用算法的设计（自适应热力学选择器）。

Abstract: Lattice reduction smooths the Gram-Schmidt profile, and we use majorization to describe the local swap mechanism behind that smoothing. In this language, each non-degenerate Lovász swap acts as a T-transform on the log-norm profile. As a consequence, every strictly Schur-convex measure of profile spread decreases at such a swap. Two structural consequences follow. First, the worst-case GSA envelope admits a variational interpretation. It is the unique minimum-variance profile compatible with the Lovász gap geometry, so its slope is determined by the LLL parameter alone. Second, the realized swap trajectory satisfies an exact telescoping identity for variance dissipation. The same viewpoint also helps organize deep-insertion heuristics. It suggests a thermal family of Schur-convex scoring rules, motivates adaptive selection within that family, and leads to two concrete selectors: Thermal-Adaptive, which reduces operation counts relative to SS-GG on flat profiles in our benchmarks while recovering SS-GG on $q$-ary inputs, and Geodesic Deep-LLL, which reduces equivalent-swap counts on structured lattices in our benchmarks at higher wall-clock cost.

</details>


### [153] [WOOTdroid: Whole-system Online On-device Tracing for Android](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27830)
*Simon Althaus,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Max Mühlhäuser,Christian Reuter,Ephraim Zimmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WOOTdroid 提出了一种无需修改操作系统或应用插桩的 Android 设备端追踪方案，通过 eBPF 实现高效系统调用审计（WDSys）和内核级 Binder 事务捕获与解码（WDBind），解决了事件丢失和语义鸿沟两大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android 系统审计面临两个核心问题：(1) 现有 syscall 追踪器在高负载下会丢失事件，当用户空间读取速度跟不上时静默覆盖条目；(2) 安全相关的应用行为通过 Binder（Android 内核 IPC 机制）传递，对 syscall 层隐藏，导致低层事件与高层行为之间的语义鸿沟。现有方案要么修改 Android 平台（难以适配系统更新），要么在用户空间插桩（易被高级攻击者绕过）。

Method: WOOTdroid 包含两个核心组件：WDSys（基于 eBPF 的 eAudit 风格 syscall 审计移植，运行于当前 Android 系统）和 WDBind（在内核层捕获 Binder parcels，通过 Java 反射提取的框架签名表在进程外解码）。整个方案无需 OS 修改或应用插桩。

Result: WDSys 在 Geekbench 上的开销最高仅为 3.6%，且比 ftrace 能追踪多 33% 的 syscall。在 Pixel 9 / Android 16 设备上的端到端案例研究中，成功重构了十项安全相关的 Binder 事务。

Conclusion: WOOTdroid 证明了在不修改操作系统或不依赖用户空间插桩的情况下，可以在 stock Android 上实现高效、完整的设备端追踪，同时解决事件丢失和语义鸿沟两大问题。

Abstract: System auditing on Android faces two problems. First, existing syscall tracers lose events under load, silently overwriting entries faster than a user space reader can drain them. Second, security-relevant application behavior is mediated through Binder, Android's kernel IPC mechanism, and is therefore hidden from the syscall layer. The Binder parcels that the kernel does see carry no method names or typed arguments, a disconnect between low-level events and high-level behavior known as the semantic gap. Existing approaches address the semantic gap either by modifying the Android platform, making them difficult to adjust to OS updates, or by instrumenting the traced application in user space, which sophisticated adversaries can evade by bypassing the instrumented framework APIs.
  We present WOOTdroid, a design and prototype for on-device tracing on stock Android that addresses both problems without OS modification or application instrumentation. WDSys, an eBPF port of eAudit-style syscall auditing, runs on current Android with at most 3.6% Geekbench overhead and traces 33% more syscalls than ftrace. WDBind captures Binder parcels in the kernel and decodes them out-of-process against a framework signature table extracted via Java reflection. We demonstrate WOOTdroid on Pixel 9 devices running Android 16 with an end-to-end case study reconstructing ten security-relevant Binder transactions.

</details>
