### [154] [TwinGate: Stateful Defense against Decompositional Jailbreaks in Untraceable Traffic via Asymmetric Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27861)
*Bowen Sun,Chaozhuo Li,Yaodong Yang,Yiwei Wang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TwinGate提出了一种基于非对称对比学习的双编码器防御框架，通过对恶意请求片段进行语义聚类和低延迟并行检测，在完全匿名化、不可追踪的分解式越狱攻击场景下实现了高恶意意图召回率和低误报率，且计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御策略在应对分解式越狱攻击时存在根本性局限：缺乏可信用户元数据时无法追踪全局历史上下文，且使用生成模型进行实时监控带来巨大的计算开销。

Method: 提出TwinGate状态化双编码器防御框架：采用非对称对比学习（ACL）将语义不同但意图匹配的恶意片段在共享隐空间中进行聚类，同时使用并行冻结编码器抑制良性主题重叠导致的误报。每个请求仅需单次轻量前向传播，防御过程可与目标模型的预填充阶段并行执行。

Result: 在包含超过362万条指令、覆盖8600个不同恶意意图的大规模数据集上，在严格因果协议下评估，TwinGate实现了高恶意意图召回率和极低的误报率，对自适应攻击具有强鲁棒性，在吞吐量和延迟方面均优于状态化和无状态基线方法。

Conclusion: TwinGate证明了在完全匿名化、不可追踪的分解式越狱攻击威胁模型下，通过轻量级双编码器结构和非对称对比学习，可以有效且高效地防御此类攻击，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Decompositional jailbreaks pose a critical threat to large language models (LLMs) by allowing adversaries to fragment a malicious objective into a sequence of individually benign queries that collectively reconstruct prohibited content. In real-world deployments, LLMs face a continuous, untraceable stream of fully anonymized and arbitrarily interleaved requests, infiltrated by covertly distributed adversarial queries. Under this rigorous threat model, state-of-the-art defensive strategies exhibit fundamental limitations. In the absence of trustworthy user metadata, they are incapable of tracking global historical contexts, while their deployment of generative models for real-time monitoring introduces computationally prohibitive overhead. To address this, we present TwinGate, a stateful dual-encoder defense framework. TwinGate employs Asymmetric Contrastive Learning (ACL) to cluster semantically disparate but intent-matched malicious fragments in a shared latent space, while a parallel frozen encoder suppresses false positives arising from benign topical overlap. Each request requires only a single lightweight forward pass, enabling the defense to execute in parallel with the target model's prefill phase at negligible latency overhead. To evaluate our approach and advance future research, we construct a comprehensive dataset of over 3.62 million instructions spanning 8,600 distinct malicious intents. Evaluated on this large-scale corpus under a strictly causal protocol, TwinGate achieves high malicious intent recall at a remarkably low false positive rate while remaining highly robust against adaptive attacks. Furthermore, our proposal substantially outperforms stateful and stateless baselines, delivering superior throughput and reduced latency.

</details>


### [155] [Latent Adversarial Detection: Adaptive Probing of LLM Activations for Multi-Turn Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28129)
*Prashant Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"对抗性不安"（adversarial restlessness）概念，指多轮提示注入攻击在模型残差流中留下的激活级信号。利用五条轨迹特征，将对话级检测率从76.2%提升至93.8%，跨四类模型家族复现。联合三源训练在混合测试集上达到89.4%检测率、2.4%假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本级防御难以检测多轮提示注入攻击中看似良性的单轮对话，需要一种基于模型内部激活信号的检测方法。

Method: 提取五条轨迹特征（总路径长度等）捕捉残差流中的对抗性不安信号，构建模型特定的检测探针，并使用三源（合成数据、LMSYS-Chat-1M、SafeDialBench）联合训练。

Result: 对话级检测率从76.2%提升至93.8%；跨四种模型家族（24B-70B）复现；三源联合训练达89.4%检测率、2.4%假阳性率；真实LMSYS数据上检测率为47-71%。

Conclusion: 对抗性不安是可靠且可泛化的激活级检测信号；二元标签不足以训练有效探针，需要三轮级标签（良性/试探/对抗）；检测器具有模型架构特异性。

Abstract: Multi-turn prompt injection follows a known attack path -- trust-building, pivoting, escalation but text-level defenses miss covert attacks where individual turns appear benign. We show this attack path leaves an activation-level signature in the model's residual stream: each phase shift moves the activation, producing a total path length far exceeding benign conversations. We call this adversarial restlessness. Five scalar trajectory features capturing this signal lift conversation-level detection from 76.2% to 93.8% on synthetic held-out data. The signal replicates across four model families (24B-70B); probes are model-specific and do not transfer across architectures. Generalization is source-dependent: leave-one-source-out evaluation shows each of synthetic, LMSYS-Chat-1M, and SafeDialBench captures distinct attack distributions, with detection on real-world LMSYS reaching 47-71% when its distribution is represented in training. Combined three-source training achieves 89.4% detection at 2.4% false positive rate on a held-out mixed set. We further show that three-phase turn-level labels(benign/pivoting/adversarial) unique to our synthetic dataset are essential: binary conversation-level labels produce 50-59% false positives. These results establish adversarial restlessness as a reliable activation-level signal and characterize the data requirements for practical deployment.

</details>


### [156] [FlashRT: Towards Computationally and Memory Efficient Red-Teaming for Prompt Injection and Knowledge Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28157)
*Yanting Wang,Chenlong Yin,Ying Chen,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlashRT是一个针对长上下文大语言模型的优化型红队攻击框架，通过计算和内存优化，在提示注入和知识污染攻击中实现2-7倍加速和2-4倍GPU内存减少，使安全评估更加高效可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化型红队攻击方法（如nanoGCG）在长上下文场景下资源消耗巨大（计算和GPU内存），阻碍了社区（尤其是学术界）对长上下文LLM安全风险进行系统性评估。

Method: FlashRT是一个首个面向长上下文LLM优化型攻击的效率提升框架，通过计算和内存两方面的优化，降低提示注入和知识污染攻击的资源开销，并兼容TAP、AutoDAN等黑盒优化方法。

Result: 相比nanoGCG基线，FlashRT实现2-7倍加速（如从1小时降至10分钟内），2-4倍GPU内存减少（如32K token上下文从264.1 GB降至65.7 GB）。

Conclusion: FlashRT可作为系统性红队工具，推动长上下文LLM安全风险评估的规模化开展，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-context large language models (LLMs)-for example, Gemini-3.1-Pro and Qwen-3.5-are widely used to empower many real-world applications, such as retrieval-augmented generation, autonomous agents, and AI assistants. However, security remains a major concern for their widespread deployment, with threats such as prompt injection and knowledge corruption. To quantify the security risks faced by LLMs under these threats, the research community has developed heuristic-based and optimization-based red-teaming methods. Optimization-based methods generally produce stronger attacks than heuristic attacks and thus provide a more rigorous assessment of LLM security risks. However, they are often resource-intensive, requiring significant computation and GPU memory, especially for long context scenarios. The resource-intensive nature poses a major obstacle for the community (especially academic researchers) to systematically evaluate the security risks of long-context LLMs and assess the effectiveness of defense strategies at scale. In this work, we propose FlashRT, the first framework to improve the efficiency (in terms of both computation and memory) for optimization-based prompt injection and knowledge corruption attacks under long-context LLMs. Through extensive evaluations, we find that FlashRT consistently delivers a 2x-7x speedup (e.g., reducing runtime from one hour to less than ten minutes) and a 2x-4x reduction in GPU memory consumption (e.g., reducing from 264.1 GB to 65.7 GB GPU memory for a 32K token context) compared to state-of-the-art baseline nanoGCG. FlashRT can be broadly applied to black-box optimization methods, such as TAP and AutoDAN. We hope FlashRT can serve as a red-teaming tool to enable systematic evaluation of long-context LLM security. The code is available at: https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [SCOPE-FE: Structured Control of Operator and Pairwise Exploration for Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27025)
*Minhee Park,Seongyeon Son,Yonghyun Lee,Eunchan Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SCOPE-FE通过结构化搜索空间控制（算子剪枝+特征聚类）来缓解自动特征工程中组合爆炸问题，在保持预测性能的同时显著降低计算开销，尤其适用于高维数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的expand-and-reduce自动特征工程方法（如OpenFE）在处理高维输入时，候选特征因算子-特征组合而呈组合爆炸式增长，导致计算成本剧增，亟需更高效的搜索空间控制策略。

Method: 提出SCOPE-FE框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）OperatorProbing：通过估计算子对数据集的效用，提前剔除低贡献算子；（2）FeatureClustering：利用谱嵌入和模糊C均值聚类将结构相关的特征分组，限制候选生成仅限于簇内组合；（3）ReliabilityScoring：引入子样本间的方差来稳定剪枝决策。

Result: 在十个基准数据集上的实验表明，SCOPE-FE显著减少了特征工程时间，同时保持与现有基线相当的预测性能；在高维数据集上的效率提升尤为突出。

Conclusion: 结构化搜索空间控制是实现可扩展的自动特征工程的有效策略，为缓解组合爆炸问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automatic feature engineering is an effective approach for improving predictive performance in tabular learning. However, expand-and-reduce methods, such as OpenFE, become increasingly computationally expensive as the input dimensionality grows. This limitation arises primarily from the combinatorial explosion of candidate features generated through operator-feature combinations. To address this issue, we propose SCOPE-FE, a structured search space control framework that improves efficiency by reducing the candidate space prior to feature generation. SCOPE-FE jointly regulates two major sources of combinatorial growth: the operator space and feature-pair space. First, OperatorProbing estimates the dataset-specific utility of candidate operators and eliminates low-contribution operators in advance. Second, FeatureClustering employs spectral embedding and fuzzy c-means clustering to group structurally related features, thereby restricting candidate generation to relevant within-cluster combinations. In addition, we introduce ReliabilityScoring, which incorporates variance across subsamples to stabilize pruning decisions. Experiments on ten benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCOPE-FE substantially reduces feature engineering time while maintaining competitive predictive performance relative to existing baselines. The efficiency gains are particularly pronounced for high-dimensional datasets. These results indicate that structured control of the search space is an effective strategy for scalable automatic feature engineering. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [158] [A Novel Computational Framework for Causal Inference: Tree-Based Discretization with ILP-Based Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27307)
*Tianyu Yang,Md. Noor-E-Alam*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合树形离散化技术和整数线性规划匹配的因果推断方法，在保证计算效率的同时，相比现有算法获得了更低的ATT估计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断方法在可解释性与计算效率之间存在权衡，需要一种既能保持高效计算又能降低估计偏差的方法。

Method: 采用面向因果推断的树形离散化技术将控制数据集在层内近似线性化，再通过整数线性规划优化框架实现全局平衡匹配。

Result: 相比最先进算法，所提方法在计算效率上更优，且ATT估计偏差更低。

Conclusion: 该混合方法有效平衡了计算效率与因果估计准确性，在实际因果推断场景中优于现有技术。

Abstract: Causal inference is essential for data-driven decision-making, as it aims to uncover causal relationships from observational data. However, identifying causality remains challenging due to the potential for confounding and the distinction between correlation and causation. While recent advances in causal machine learning and matching algorithms have improved estimation accuracy, these methods often face trade-offs between interpretability and computational efficiency. This paper proposes a novel approach that combines a tree-based discretization technique, tailored for causal inference, with an integer linear programming-based matching algorithm. The discretization ensures approximately linear relationships for control datasets within strata, enabling effective matching, while the optimization framework optimizes for global balance. The resulting algorithm yields computational efficiency and less biased ATT estimates compared to state-of-the-art algorithms. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the proposed method's practical advantages over existing techniques in causal inference scenarios.

</details>


### [159] [Bayesian X-Learner: Calibrated Posterior Inference for Heterogeneous Treatment Effects under Heavy-Tailed Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27394)
*Eichi Uehara*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯X-Learner，首次在条件平均处理效应(CATE)估计中同时实现三个特性：异质性效应、校准的不确定性以及对重尾污染数据的鲁棒性。该方法通过交叉拟合双重稳健伪结果、Welsch重下降伪似然的完整MCMC后验以及Huber-δ污染扩展来实现，在IHDP基准上表现有竞争力，在污染数据场景下RMSE≈0.13且可信区间覆盖率达83-95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CATE估计方法无法同时满足三个实际需求：异质性效应、校准的不确定性和对重尾污染数据的鲁棒性。元学习器能给出异质性效应但缺乏不确定性量化；因果森林和BART能提供前两者但依赖高斯尾部假设；没有广泛使用的工具能同时满足所有三个特性。

Method: 基于X-Learner框架，使用交叉拟合的双重稳健伪结果(Kennedy, 2020)，引入Welsch重下降伪似然进行完整MCMC后验推断，得到τ(x)的后验分布。针对污染数据，提出contamination_severity扩展，通过Huber的minimax-δ关系选择Huber-δ nuisance损失，并采用模块化贝叶斯池化结合贝叶斯bootstrap nuisance抽样恢复名义覆盖。

Result: 在Hill's IHDP基准上，默认配置5次重复的均方根PEHE均值为0.56（最低均值），但与S-/T-/X-learners、Causal BART和因果森林的差异在α=0.05水平不显著。在20-25%尾部密度的污染"whale" DGP上，RMSE≈0.13，30次种子单交叉拟合覆盖率为83%（20%密度），通过模块化贝叶斯池化可恢复95%名义覆盖。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯X-Learner是首个能同时提供异质性效应、校准不确定性和重尾鲁棒性的广泛可用工具，在标准基准上具有竞争力，在污染数据上表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE) estimation in practice demands three properties simultaneously: heterogeneous effects $τ(x)$, calibrated uncertainty over them, and robustness to the heavy tails that contaminate real outcome data. Meta-learners (Künzel et al., 2019) give (i); causal forests and BART give (i)-(ii) with Gaussian-tail assumptions; no widely used tool gives all three. We present Bayesian X-Learner, an X-Learner built on cross-fitted doubly robust pseudo-outcomes (Kennedy, 2020) with a full MCMC posterior over $τ(x)$ via a Welsch redescending pseudo-likelihood. On Hill's IHDP benchmark the default configuration attains mean $\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{PEHE}}} = 0.56$ on 5 replications (lowest mean; differences from S-/T-/X-learners, full-config Causal BART, and a causal forest baseline are not significant at $α=0.05$, and rank ordering is unstable at 10 replications -- IHDP comparisons are competitive rather than dominant). On contaminated "whale" DGPs with up to 20-25% tail density, a one-flag extension (contamination_severity) that selects a Huber-$δ$ nuisance loss per Huber's minimax-$δ$ relation recovers RMSE $\approx 0.13$ with tight credible intervals (single-cross-fit 30-seed coverage 83% [Wilson 66%, 93%] at 20% density; modular-Bayes pooling with Bayesian-bootstrap nuisance draws restores nominal 95% coverage).

</details>


### [160] [Prediction-powered Inference by Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27892)
*Yanwu Gu,Linglong Kong,Dong Xia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合专家（MOE）的预测驱动半监督推断框架，通过优化专家权重以最小化方差，在标签稀缺、无标签数据丰富的场景下，实现比标准预测驱动推断（PPI）更高效的统计推断，并适用于均值估计、线性回归、分位数估计及M估计等多种任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能模型日益多样化的背景下，有标签数据昂贵且稀缺，而无标签数据丰富易得。现有预测工具各自具备不同优势，如何有效整合这些预测器的集体预测能力来提升半监督推断的效率是一个关键问题。标准PPI方法使用单一预测器，无法自适应地利用多个预测器的优势。

Method: 将多个预测器视为混合专家（MOE），基于预测驱动推断（PPI）的方差缩减原理，构建一个MOE-powered半监督推断框架。该框架通过优化专家混合权重，使得加权后的预测器组合达到最小方差，从而提升推断效率。

Result: 建立了非渐近理论，给出了置信区间覆盖误差的上界。数值实验验证了该框架相比标准PPI具有更好的实际效果，能够自适应地利用多个预测器的优势，并享受最佳专家的性能保障。

Conclusion: MOE-powered推断框架是一种灵活、高效且具有理论保障的半监督推断方法，适用于均值估计、线性回归、分位数估计及一般M估计等多种任务，在有限标签场景下具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: The rapidly expanding artificial intelligence (AI) industry has produced diverse yet powerful prediction tools, each with its own network architecture, training strategy, data-processing pipeline, and domain-specific strengths. These tools create new opportunities for semi-supervised inference, in which labeled data are limited and expensive to obtain, whereas unlabeled data are abundant and widely available. Given a collection of predictors, we treat them as a mixture of experts (MOE) and introduce an MOE-powered semi-supervised inference framework built upon prediction-powered inference (PPI). Motivated by the variance reduction principle underlying PPI, the proposed framework seeks the mixture of experts that achieves the smallest possible variance. Compared with standard PPI, the MOE-powered inference framework adapts to the unknown performance of individual predictors, benefits from their collective predictive power, and enjoys a best-expert guarantee. The framework is flexible and applies to mean estimation, linear regression, quantile estimation, and general M-estimation. We develop non-asymptotic theory for the MOE-powered inference framework and establish upper bounds on the coverage error of the resulting confidence intervals. Numerical experiments demonstrate the practical effectiveness of MOE-powered inference and corroborate our theoretical findings.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Adaptive Robust Confidence Intervals in Efron's Gaussian Two-Groups Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26992)
*Qiaosen Wang,Shuwen Chai,Chao Gao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文在Efron高斯二群组模型下研究鲁棒不确定性量化，刻画了对抗未知污染比例ε时置信区间的最优长度，发现其比已知ε时显著更长（σ(n^{-1/4}+ε^{1/2}/max{1, log(en ε^2)}^{1/2})），并在方差未知时进一步退化至Ω(σn^{-1/8})。算法上提出基于傅里叶和Carathéodory正半定约束的认证程序，达到极小极大下界且多项式时间可计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际实验科学中，污染更多是在测量前由上游过程引入（即噪声不可知对抗），而非测量时的人为破坏。现有的Huber模型假设任意比例污染，但未考虑这种噪声不可知特性。本文旨在研究在此更符合实际的设定下，对未知污染比例自适应时置信区间长度的极小极大最优性。

Method: 理论方面，刻画了已知/未知污染比例下置信区间长度的极小极大最优率，并分析方差已知/未知两种情形。算法方面，提出基于傅里叶分析的认证程序，利用Carathéodory正半定约束构造残差特征函数与高斯位置混合的一致性检验，通过扫描候选点并接受符合条件的点来构建置信区间。

Result: 方差已知时，对未知ε自适应的最优置信区间长度为σ(n^{-1/4}+ε^{1/2}/max{1, log(en ε^2)}^{1/2})，比已知ε时（σn^{-1/2}）多项式地更差。方差未知时，进一步退化为Ω(σn^{-1/8})。所提算法达到该极小极大下界。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化中的鲁棒自适应会因污染比例未知而付出显著代价，这一代价在方差未知时更为严重。所提出的傅里叶认证算法在理论上是最优的且计算可行。

Abstract: Robust uncertainty quantification is increasingly important in modern data analysis and is often formalized under Huber's model, which allows an $\varepsilon$-fraction of arbitrary corruptions. In many experimental sciences, however, the measurement protocol is well controlled, and contamination is more plausibly introduced upstream. Motivated by this noise-oblivious nature of adversaries, we study confidence intervals for the null location parameter $θ$ in Efron's Gaussian two-groups model, where an unknown fraction $\varepsilon$ of observations have arbitrarily shifted means, but all samples share the same law of additive Gaussian measurement noise with variance $σ^2$. We characterize the minimax-optimal length among confidence intervals with a prescribed coverage level uniformly over the unknown contamination proportion and all noise-oblivious adversaries. Although prior work has shown that the minimax point estimation rate of theta does not deteriorate when $\varepsilon$ becomes unknown, our results reveal that, with a given $σ^2$, the minimax-optimal length of confidence intervals that are adaptive to unknown $\varepsilon$ is of order $σ(n^{-1/4}+\varepsilon^{1/2}/\max\{1, \log(en \varepsilon^2)\}^{1/2})$, which is polynomially worse than the optimal length when $\varepsilon$ is known. When the variance $σ^2$ is also unknown, we show a further degradation: no adaptive robust confidence interval can be shorter than $Ω(σn^{-1/8})$. Algorithmically, we introduce a Fourier-based certification procedure built on Carathéodory's positive-semidefiniteness constraints. By scanning candidate points and accepting those whose residual characteristic function is certifiably consistent with a Gaussian location mixture, our algorithm attains the minimax lower bound in the known-variance setting and is computable in polynomial time.

</details>


### [162] [The Bernstein-von Mises theorem for Bayesian one-pass online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27442)
*Jeyong Lee,Junhyeok Choi,Dongguen Kim,Minwoo Chae*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对单次遍历（one-pass）设置的贝叶斯在线学习算法，该算法通过引入预热启动阶段确保稳定序贯更新，证明了后验分布达到最优收敛率，并建立了在线版本的Bernstein-von Mises定理，实现无需发散小批量样本大小的有效不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯在线学习的理论保证通常要求小批量样本大小发散，这在单次遍历设置中无法满足，导致其理论理解受限。本文旨在填补这一空白，为单次遍历场景提供具有理论保障的贝叶斯在线学习算法。

Method: 提出一种包含预热启动阶段的新型贝叶斯在线学习算法，以稳定序贯更新过程。基于全新的理论分析框架，证明后验分布的最优收敛率，并推导在线版本的Bernstein-von Mises定理。

Result: 所提算法在广义线性模型上的数值实验表明，其性能与批估计器相当，且优于现有的在线算法。理论上证明了后验的最优收敛率和不确定性量化的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为单次遍历贝叶斯在线学习提供了严格的理论基础，证明了无需发散小批量样本大小即可实现最优收敛和有效不确定性量化，所提算法具有良好的实用性能。

Abstract: Bayesian online learning provides a coherent framework for sequential inference. However, its theoretical understanding remains limited, particularly in the one-pass setting. Existing theoretical guarantees typically require the mini-batch sample size to diverge, a condition that fails in the one-pass regime. In this paper, we propose a new Bayesian online learning algorithm tailored to the one-pass setting, which incorporates a warm-start phase to ensure stable sequential updates. For this algorithm, we show that the sequentially updated posterior attains the optimal convergence rate. Building on this, we establish an online analogue of the Bernstein-von Mises theorem, which guarantees valid uncertainty quantification without diverging mini-batch sample sizes. Our analysis is based on a novel theoretical framework that differs fundamentally from existing approaches in the online learning literature. Numerical experiments on generalized linear models show that the proposed method matches the performance of the batch estimator while outperforming existing online procedures.

</details>


### [163] [Technical Note on Relating Scores of Tilted Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27196)
*Curtis McDonald*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文扩展了线性倾斜与负对角二次倾斜下得分函数与卷积之间的关系，证明了倾斜密度下的得分可以通过原始卷积过程在位置和噪声水平上的平移来理解，为基于得分的扩散模型提供了新的理论视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解倾斜（tilt）对参考测度的得分函数的影响，特别是线性倾斜和负对角二次倾斜情形下得分函数的表达形式，为扩散模型中的得分估计提供理论支持。

Method: 利用Tweedie公式将去噪器与得分函数关联起来，分别分析线性倾斜和二次倾斜对得分算子的影响：线性倾斜导致位置偏移，二次倾斜同时导致位置偏移和时间偏移。通过卷积关系建立原始密度与倾斜密度得分函数之间的联系。

Result: 证明了在给定原始密度的卷积过程下，倾斜密度的得分函数可以通过改变位置和噪声水平从原始卷积过程的得分函数中导出。

Conclusion: 这些倾斜-得分关系对基于得分的扩散模型社区有价值，可用于构建更好的得分估计器。

Abstract: Recent results have shown that for a linear tilt to a reference measure, the scores that would be produced under convolution with a normal variable can be expressed in terms of convolutions of the original density. Here, we extend that result to include constant negative diagonal tilts as well. The relationship follows from relating the denoisers of the two densities, which define the scores via Tweedie formula. A linear tilt results in a location shift to the score operator, while a quadratic tilt results in both a location shift and a time shift. Thus the scores of the tilted density can be understood as the scores of the original convolution process at a different location and noise level. These results are of interest to those in the score based diffusion community, and may lead to better score estimators which take advantage of these tilted score relationships.

</details>


### [164] [A note on estimation of quarticity based on spot volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27665)
*Yi Guo*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对连续伊藤半鞅的四次变差估计问题，提出了一种更直观的估计量，建立了其在稳定收敛意义下以 $1/\sqrt{\Delta_n}$ 速率收敛的中心极限定理，并与现有估计量的渐近方差进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四次变差估计量较为复杂，本文旨在提出一种更直观的估计方法，并严格建立其渐近分布理论，以提升可解释性和实用性。

Method: 提出一种直观的四次变差估计量，利用离散观测数据构造；通过稳定收敛理论推导其中心极限定理，收敛速率为 $1/\sqrt{\Delta_n}$；并与经典估计量的渐近方差进行理论对比。

Result: 新估计量在 $1/\sqrt{\Delta_n}$ 速率下满足稳定收敛的中心极限定理，且其渐近方差与现有估计量存在差异，文中给出了具体的比较结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的直观估计量在渐近性质上具有竞争力，为连续伊藤半鞅的四次变差估计提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we aim at estimating the quarticity of continuous Itô semimartingales. Instead of using some classical estimators, we introduce a more intuitive one and establish a central limit theorem (CLT) for it, with a convergence rate of $1/\sqrt{Δ_n}$ in the sense of stable convergence. Moreover, we compare the asymptotic variance of this estimator with that of other existing estimators.

</details>


### [165] [A High Dimensional Wild Bootstrap Max-Test for Detecting the Presence of Significant Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27813)
*Jonathan B. Hill*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于块自助法的最大值检验（max-test），用于在高维弱依赖异质数据中检测显著预测变量，避免协方差矩阵估计和Bonferroni校正，通过乘子块自助法近似非标准极限分布，在数值实验和VIX波动率指数实证中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维时间序列回归中，存在弱依赖、非平稳数据以及p远大于n的情况，传统方法需要协方差矩阵估计或自适应重采样，且面临非均匀渐近性和多重比较校正问题。本文旨在构建一种无需协方差矩阵估计和Bonferroni校正的检验方法，能够处理指数级增长的协变量数量，并适用于弱依赖和异质数据。

Method: 构造基于最大统计量的块自助最大值检验（max-test），在边际回归框架下利用物理依赖（physical dependence）刻画时间序列的相关结构。采用乘子（wild）块自助法来逼近由大量估计量最大值产生的非标准极限分布，完全绕过协方差矩阵估计和自适应重采样步骤。

Result: 该max-test在控制检验尺寸方面表现良好，能够有效检测各种偏离原假设的情形，包括弱信号或稀疏信号的微小偏离。数值实验和VIX波动率指数的实证分析验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的块自助最大值检验为高维时间序列中的变量筛选提供了一种稳健且计算可行的解决方案，无需协方差矩阵估计和多重比较校正，在弱依赖异质数据下具有良好的有限样本性能。

Abstract: We construct a block bootstrap max-test for detecting the presence of significant predictors in a high dimensional setting, allowing for weakly dependent and heterogeneous (possibly non-stationary) data. The number of covariates to be screened may be large $p$ $>>$ $n$, and growing at an exponential rate, provided $\ln (p)$ $=$ $o(n^{a})$ for some $a$ $>$ $0$ that depends on memory decay and the growth of higher moments. We study the problem of correlation screening in a high dimensional marginal regression setting, assuming so-called \textit{physical dependence} in a time series setting. We entirely sidestep covariance matrix estimation and adaptive re-sampling by working with a max-statistic over the many computed parameters. Thus we do not need endogenous selection of the most relevant predictor index yielding non-uniform asymptotics, nor do we need a post-estimation Bonferroni correction. The non-standard limit distribution arising from the maximum of an increasing number of estimators is easily approximated by a multiplier (wild) block bootstrap. The max-test controls for size well, performs well against various deviations from the null, including very slight deviations with a weak or sparse signal. A numerical experiment is performed and an empirical example with the VIX volatility index is provided.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Learning Rate Transfer in Normalized Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27077)
*Boris Shigida,Boris Hanin,Andrey Gromov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出νGPT，一种新的nGPT参数化方法，通过结合对齐指数修改μP超参数迁移方法，实现了学习率在宽度、深度和Token序列长度上的有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nGPT虽然训练加速效果好且无需权重衰减和学习率预热，但其超参数无法在模型维度和Token序列长度上实现学习率迁移，这限制了其在不同规模模型间的泛化应用。

Method: 结合数值实验与对齐指数的理论框架，重新审视并修改μP的超参数迁移方法，提出新的νGPT参数化方案。

Result: νGPT在宽度、深度和Token序列长度三个维度上均实现了学习率的有效迁移。

Conclusion: 通过理论指导的参数化改进，νGPT克服了nGPT在超参数迁移上的局限性，为大规模模型训练提供了更鲁棒的参数化方案。

Abstract: The Normalized Transformer, or nGPT (arXiv:2410.01131) achieves impressive training speedups and does not require weight decay or learning rate warmup. However, despite having hyperparameters that explicitly scale with model size, we observe that nGPT does not exhibit learning rate transfer across model dimension and token horizon. To rectify this, we combine numerical experiments with a principled use of alignment exponents (arXiv:2407.05872) to revisit and modify the $μ$P approach to hyperparameter transfer (arXiv:2011.14522). The result is a novel nGPT parameterization we call $ν$GPT. Through extensive empirical validation, we find $ν$GPT exhibits learning rate transfer across width, depth, and token horizon.

</details>


### [167] [Optimized Deferral for Imbalanced Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27723)
*Corinna Cortes,Anqi Mao,Mehryar Mohri,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了两阶段学习延迟（Learning to Defer）中的专家不平衡问题，提出了一种基于边界的代价敏感学习方法MILD，通过将延迟损失优化转化为输入-专家域上的代价敏感学习问题，设计了新的边界损失函数和算法，在图像分类和LLM路由任务中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段学习延迟方法面临专家不平衡问题，即多数专家主导了延迟决策，导致算法性能次优。亟需一种能够有效处理专家不平衡的延迟学习框架。

Method: 将延迟损失优化转化为输入-专家域上的代价敏感学习问题；推导了适用于该设定的新边界损失函数及其理论保证；开发了新的代价敏感学习算法；基于这些结果设计了MILD算法。

Result: 在图像分类和真实世界大语言模型路由任务上的大量实验表明，MILD相比现有基线方法取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的代价敏感学习视角以及MILD算法有效解决了专家不平衡问题，为两阶段学习延迟提供了理论完备且实践有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning algorithms can be significantly improved by routing complex or uncertain inputs to specialized experts, balancing accuracy with computational cost. This approach, known as learning to defer, is essential in domains like natural language generation, medical diagnosis, and computer vision, where an effective deferral can reduce errors at low extra resource consumption. However, the two-stage learning to defer setting, which leverages existing predictors such as a collection of LLMs or other classifiers, often faces challenges due to an expert imbalance problem. This imbalance can lead to suboptimal performance, with deferral algorithms favoring the majority expert. We present a comprehensive study of two-stage learning to defer in expert imbalance settings. We cast the deferral loss optimization as a novel cost-sensitive learning problem over the input-expert domain. We derive new margin-based loss functions and guarantees tailored to this setting, and develop novel algorithms for cost-sensitive learning. Leveraging these results, we design principled deferral algorithms, MILD (Margin-based Imbalanced Learning to Defer), specifically suited for expert imbalance settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, showing clear improvements over existing baselines on both image classification and real-world Large Language Model (LLM) routing tasks.

</details>


### [168] [Linear-Core Surrogates: Smooth Loss Functions with Linear Rates for Classification and Structured Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27742)
*Mehryar Mohri,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Linear-Core (LC) Surrogates，一类通过在平滑尾部拼接线性核心构造的新型凸损失函数，在保持处处可微的同时实现严格的线性H一致性界，解决了分类中损失函数平滑性与统计效率之间的根本矛盾。该方法在结构化预测中可实现无偏随机梯度估计，避免二次复杂度推理，实验获得23倍加速，并在噪声标签场景下比Cross-Entropy提升2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类损失函数存在根本性权衡：平滑损失（如交叉熵）优化速度快但一致性速率慢（平方根），分段线性损失（如合页损失）一致性速率快（线性）但不可微。本文旨在设计一种同时兼顾两者优点的损失函数，即为可微且保有线性一致性速率。

Method: 提出Linear-Core (LC) Surrogates，将线性核心与平滑尾部拼接构造凸损失函数，证明其处处可微且保留严格线性H一致性界。在结构化预测中利用该平滑性设计无偏随机梯度估计器，绕过精确推理的O(|Y|^2)二次复杂度。

Result: 在大词汇序列标注任务中实现23倍加速于结构化SVM；在损坏的CIFAR-10上比交叉熵高出2.6%，展现对实例相关标签噪声的优越鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LC Surrogates成功解决了分类中平滑性与统计效率的权衡问题，兼具可微性与线性一致性速率，并在大规模结构化预测和噪声标签场景下展现出显著的实用优势。

Abstract: The choice of loss function in classification involves a fundamental trade-off: smooth losses (like Cross-Entropy) enable fast optimization rates but yield slow square-root consistency bounds, while piecewise-linear losses (like Hinge) offer fast linear consistency rates but suffer from non-differentiability. We propose Linear-Core (LC) Surrogates, a new family of convex loss functions that resolve this tension by stitching a linear core to a smooth tail. We prove that these surrogates are differentiable everywhere while retaining strict linear $H$-consistency bounds, effectively combining the optimization benefits of smoothness with the statistical efficiency of margin-based losses. In the structured prediction setting, we show that this smoothness unlocks a massive computational and energy advantage: it allows for an unbiased stochastic gradient estimator that bypasses the quadratic complexity $O(|\mathscr{Y}|^2)$ of exact inference (e.g., Viterbi). Empirically, our method achieves a 23$\times$ speedup over Structured SVMs on large-vocabulary sequence tagging tasks and demonstrates superior robustness to instance-dependent label noise, outperforming Cross-Entropy by 2.6% on corrupted CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [169] [Monitoring Neural Training with Topology: A Footprint-Predictable Collapse Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26984)
*Alexander Kalinowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于拓扑感知的在线监测方法（MMHM+CI），通过稀疏编辑和离散Morse匹配实现低延迟的表征坍塌早期预警，适用于LLM微调和时序KGE训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征坍塌（嵌入变得各向异性、失去多尺度结构）会在性能指标下降之前就侵蚀下游表现，现有方法缺乏在线、低延迟的早期检测手段。

Method: 提出Modular Morse Homology Maintenance (MMHM) 与复合Collapse Index (CI) 结合的在线监测框架：不重建复形，而是以固定尺度进行稀疏编辑并维护离散Morse匹配，实现增量更新。

Result: 在LLM微调和时序知识图谱嵌入训练中，CI提供了低延迟的早期预警信号，适合在训练过程中进行干预。

Conclusion: MMHM+CI方法能够有效监测表征坍塌，为训练中的早期干预提供可靠信号。

Abstract: Representational collapse, where embeddings become anisotropic and lose multi-scale structure, can erode downstream performance long before performance metrics react. We propose an online, topology-aware monitor for evolving neural representations that couples Modular Morse Homology Maintenance (MMHM) with a composite Collapse Index (CI). Instead of rebuilding complexes each epoch, we apply sparse edits at a fixed scale and maintain a discrete Morse matching, yielding fast, incremental updates. Across LLM fine-tuning and temporal KGE training, CI provides a low-latency early-warning signal suitable for in-training interventions. Code and experimental scripts will be released publicly

</details>


### [170] [Simple Self-Conditioning Adaptation for Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26985)
*Michael Cardei,Huu Binh Ta,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自条件掩码扩散模型（SCMDM），通过让每个去噪步骤基于模型自身之前的干净状态预测进行条件化，无需额外训练或推理开销，即可显著提升掩码扩散模型的生成质量。在多个领域（文本、图像、分子、基因组）实验中，SCMDM均优于标准掩码扩散基线，生成困惑度降低近50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码扩散模型中，若某位置在反向更新后仍处于掩码状态，模型会丢弃对该位置的干净状态预测，导致仍需掩码的位置只能从掩码token本身反复推断，限制了跨步的精细化能力。

Method: 提出SCMDM，一种训练后适配方法：在每个去噪步骤中，将模型自身上一轮生成的干净状态预测作为条件输入。仅需最小架构改动，无需循环隐状态通路、无需辅助参考模型、无需额外的去噪器评估，也无需从头训练模型。

Result: 在多个领域（OWT文本、离散图像、小分子、基因组）上均一致超越标准MDM基线。在OWT上生成困惑度从42.89降至23.72（降幅近50%），图像合成质量、小分子生成、基因组分布建模的保真度均显著提升。

Conclusion: 训练后自条件化是一种高效且有效的掩码扩散模型增强方法；部分自条件化策略（包括50% dropout）在训练后场景中并非最优，当模型自生成的干净状态估计变得有信息量时，专门进行精细化优于混合条件与非条件目标。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate discrete sequences by iterative denoising under an absorbing masking process. In standard masked diffusion, if a token remains masked after a reverse update, the model discards its clean-state prediction for that position. Thus, still-masked positions must be repeatedly inferred from the mask token alone. This design choice limits cross-step refinement. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a simple, yet effective, post-training adaptation for MDMs that conditions each denoising step on the model's own previous clean-state predictions. The resulting method, called Self-Conditioned Masked Diffusion Models (SCMDM), requires minimal architectural change, does not introduce a recurrent latent-state pathway, does not rely on an auxiliary reference model, and adds no extra denoiser evaluations during sampling. This is an important departure from partial self-conditioning approaches which requires expensive model training from scratch. In particular, the paper shows that partial self-conditioning, including the commonly used 50% dropout strategy for training self-conditioned models from scratch, is suboptimal in the post-training regime. Instead, once the model's self-generated clean-state estimates become informative, the specialization to refinement is preferable to mixing conditional and unconditional objectives. SCMDM is evaluated across multiple domains, demonstrating consistent improvement over vanilla MDM baselines, achieving nearly a 50% reduction in generative perplexity on OWT-trained models (42.89 to 23.72), alongside strong improvements in discretized image synthesis quality, small molecular generation, and enhanced fidelity in genomic distribution modeling.

</details>


### [171] [People-Centred Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26991)
*Zheng Zhang,Milad Masroor,Cuong Nguyen,Tahir Hassan,Yuanhong Chen,David Rosewarne,Kevin Wells,Thanh-Toan Do,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出人本医学图像分析框架PecMan，通过动态门控机制在 clinician 工作负载约束下联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流效率，并引入FairHAI基准评估准确率-公平性-临床工作量之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的医学AI虽然诊断准确率高，但临床采纳率低；原因在于忽视了跨人群的公平性优化和工作流整合。先前的工作流整合（如L2D、L2C）与AI公平性研究相互孤立，忽略了它们的天然依赖关系以及临床环境中医生可用性受限等实际约束。

Method: 提出人本医学图像分析框架PecMan，采用动态门控机制，在 clinician 工作量约束下将病例分配给AI、医生或两者协作，同时优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流效率。

Result: 在提出的FairHAI基准上，PecMan在准确率、公平性和临床工作量之间的权衡上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PecMan通过联合优化公平性、准确性和工作流整合，为更可信、临床可行的AI系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-centric medical AI have produced highly accurate diagnostic systems, but the emphasis on data curation and performance metrics has not translated into widespread clinical adoption. We conjecture that this limited uptake stems from insufficient attention dedicated to the optimisation of fair performance across diverse patient populations and to workflow integration: performance biases can create regulatory barriers, and poorly integrated automation can disrupt clinical routines, degrade the quality of human-AI collaboration, and reduce clinicians' willingness to adopt AI tools. Prior work on workflow integration (e.g., Learning to Defer (L2D) and Learning to Complement (L2C)) and AI fairness has typically examined these challenges in isolation, overlooking their natural interdependence and the practical constraints of clinical environments, such as restricted clinician availability. We propose People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan), a human-AI framework that jointly optimises fairness, diagnostic accuracy, and workflow effectiveness through a dynamic gating mechanism that assigns cases to AI, clinicians, or both under clinician workload constraints. We also introduce the Fairness and Human-Centred AI (FairHAI) benchmark for evaluating trade-offs between accuracy, fairness, and clinician workload. Experiments using this benchmark show that PecMan consistently outperforms existing methods, paving the way for more trustworthy and clinically viable AI systems. Code will be available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [172] [Automatic Causal Fairness Analysis with LLM-Generated Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27011)
*Alessia Berarducci,Eric Rossetto,Alessandro Antonucci,Marco Zaffalon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FairMind原型系统，通过在数据集层面利用标准公平性模型的因果反事实推理进行自动化公平性分析，并借助LLM生成详细报告，同时针对有序保护变量和连续目标进行了扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoML框架大多忽视了训练数据及预测结果中潜在的公平性问题，缺乏系统化的公平性分析机制。

Method: 基于Plečko和Bareinboim提出的标准公平性模型，利用反事实查询对保护特征的不同取值进行因果效应计算；采用闭式公式进行效应计算，并在零样本设置下利用LLM生成公平性分析报告。

Result: 通过示例展示了FairMind相比直接使用LLM进行公平性分析的预期优势，并提出了针对有序保护变量和连续目标的新型分解结果。

Conclusion: FairMind提供了一个在数据集层面进行自动化、因果导向的公平性评估的可行框架，结合LLM增强了报告的可解释性，并拓展了公平性分析的应用场景。

Abstract: AutoML, intended as the process of automating the application of machine learning to real-world problems, is a key step for AI popularisation. Most AutoML frameworks are not accounting for the potential lack of fairness in the training data and in the corresponding predictions. We introduce \textsc{FairMind}, a software prototype aiming to automatise fairness analysis at the dataset level. We achieve that by resorting to the assumptions of the \emph{standard fairness model}, recently proposed by Plečko and Bareinboim. This allows for a sound fairness evaluation in terms of causal effects, based on \emph{counterfactual} queries involving the target, possibly confounders and mediators, and the different values of an input feature we regard as \emph{protected}. After the necessary data preprocessing, the tool implements a closed-form computation of the effects. LLMs are consequently exploited to generate accurate reports on the fairness levels detected in the training dataset. We achieve that in a zero-shot setup and show by examples the expected advantages with respect to a direct analysis performed by the LLM. To favour applications, extensions to ordinal protected variable and continuous targets and novel decomposition results are also discussed.

</details>


### [173] [Fidelity, Diversity, and Privacy: A Multi-Dimensional LLM Evaluation for Clinical Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27014)
*Guillermo Iglesias,Gema Bello-Orgaz,María Navas-Loro,Cristian Ramirez-Atencia,Mercè Salvador Robert,Enrique Baca-Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对心理健康领域高质量标注数据稀缺的问题，本文提出利用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5等大语言模型，基于ICD-10编码生成合成心理健康评估报告，并从语义保真度、词汇多样性和隐私/抄袭三个维度进行评估，证明该方法能生成临床连贯、多样且隐私安全的合成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康领域高质量标注医疗数据极度稀缺，且隐私法规严格限制数据共享，成为训练鲁棒机器学习模型的主要瓶颈，迫切需要一种既能扩充数据又保护患者隐私的替代方案。

Method: 使用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5三种大语言模型，以ICD-10编码为条件生成合成心理健康评估报告；引入包含语义保真度、词汇多样性、隐私/抄袭检测三个维度的综合评估框架，以防范模式坍塌或隐私泄露风险。

Result: 所有三种模型均能生成临床连贯、词汇多样且隐私安全的合成诊断报告，有效扩充了临床自然语言处理任务的可训练数据。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的合成数据生成方法可在不损害患者保密性的前提下，显著扩充心理健康领域的训练数据，为隐私受限的医疗NLP任务提供了可行的数据增强方案。

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated medical data, particularly in mental health, poses a significant bottleneck for training robust machine learning models. Privacy regulations restrict data sharing, making synthetic data generation a promising alternative. The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in a data augmentation pipeline could be leveraged as an alternative in this field. In the proposed methodology, DeepSeek-R1, OpenBioLLM-Llama3 and Qwen 3.5 are used to generate synthetic mental health evaluation reports conditioned on specific International Classification of Diseases, Tenth Revision (ICD-10) codes. Because naive text generation can lead to mode collapse or privacy breaches (memorization), a comprehensive evaluation framework is introduced. The generated diagnostic texts are assessed across three dimensions: semantic fidelity, lexical diversity, and privacy/plagiarism. The results demonstrate that all models can generate clinically coherent, diverse, and privacy-safe synthetic reports, significantly expanding the available training data for clinical natural language processing tasks without compromising patient confidentiality.

</details>


### [174] [Cross-Subject Generalization for EEG Decoding: A Survey of Deep Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27033)
*Taida Li,Yujun Yan,Fei Dou,Wenzhan Song,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了针对跨被试EEG解码中个体差异导致的域迁移问题，系统分类了特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习等方法，并探讨了当前方法的理论局限、被试身份的结构价值及EEG基础模型的兴起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在跨被试EEG解码中面临严重的个体间域迁移问题，限制了模型对未见被试的泛化能力，亟需系统性方法论梳理。

Method: 将跨被试EEG解码形式化为多源域问题，建立严格的被试独立评估协议，并基于特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦、对比学习四个方法论家族对现有文献进行系统分类与综述。

Result: 形成了覆盖四大方法论家族的系统性分类体系，并识别出当前方法的理论局限、被试身份信息的结构性价值以及EEG基础模型三个关键发展方向。

Conclusion: 跨被试EEG解码的进步需要突破现有方法的理论瓶颈，重新审视被试身份信息的作用，并积极拥抱EEG基础模型这一新兴范式。

Abstract: Deep learning for cross-subject EEG decoding is hindered by high inter-subject variability, which introduces a severe domain shift between training and unseen test subjects. This survey presents a comprehensive review of deep learning methodologies specifically engineered to address this cross-subject generalization challenge. To ground this analysis, we formalize the cross-subject setting as a multi-source domain problem and delineate the rigorous, subject-independent evaluation protocols required for valid assessment. Central to this survey is a systematic taxonomy of the current literature into discrete methodological families, including feature alignment, adversarial learning, feature disentanglement, and contrastive learning. We conclude by examining three critical elements for advancing robust, real-world decoding: the theoretical limitations of current methodologies, the structural value of subject identity, and the emergence of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [175] [Detecting Clinical Discrepancies in Health Coaching Agents: A Dual-Stream Memory and Reconciliation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27045)
*Samuel L Pugh,Eric Yang,Alexander Muir Sutherland,Alessandra Breschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双流记忆架构（Dual-Stream Memory Architecture），严格分离患者自述与结构化临床记录（FHIR），通过专门的协调引擎（Reconciliation Engine）评估每一条提取的记忆与FHIR档案的差异，在675次纵向健康辅导会话中检测出84.4%的临床差异，并发现了13.6%的错误级联现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体从单会话工具向管理长期医疗旅程的持久系统过渡时，现有通用记忆系统倾向于用患者最新陈述覆盖旧事实以保持连贯性，这种模式在临床数据中存在安全隐患。需要解决患者自述（实时但易回忆偏差）与电子健康记录（经医学验证但常过时）两种不完美信息源的矛盾。

Method: 提出双流记忆架构：1) 严格分离患者叙事流与结构化临床记录流（FHIR格式）；2) 引入协调引擎，对每一条从会话中提取的记忆进行FHIR档案比对，按类型、严重程度及涉及的FHIR资源对差异进行分类；3) 在26名患者、675次纵向健康辅导会话的混合数据集（真实医患对话+合成FHIR临床场景）上评估。

Result: 协调引擎在隔离测试中检测出84.4%的设计临床差异，安全关键召回率达86.7%。通过将提取与协调评估耦合测试，直接量化出13.6%的错误级联，追溯发现退化主要源于从非结构化对话中提取记忆时丢失的临床细节，而非下游分类错误。

Conclusion: 验证了将患者报告的记忆与临床记录进行验证不仅是可行的，而且是纵向健康智能体安全部署的必要条件。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents transition from single-session tools to persistent systems managing longitudinal healthcare journeys, their memory architectures face a critical challenge: reconciling two imperfect sources of truth. The patient's evolving self-report is current but prone to recall bias, while the Electronic Health Record (EHR) is medically validated but frequently stale. General-purpose agent memory systems optimize for coherence by overwriting older facts with the user's latest statement, a pattern that risks safety failures when applied to clinical data. We introduce a Dual-Stream Memory Architecture that strictly separates the patient narrative from the structured clinical record (FHIR), governed by a dedicated Reconciliation Engine that evaluates every extracted memory against the patient's FHIR profile and classifies discrepancies by type, severity, and the specific FHIR resources involved. We evaluate this architecture on 26 patients across 675 longitudinal wellness coaching sessions, using a hybrid dataset that interleaves real provider-patient transcripts with synthetic, FHIR-grounded clinical scenarios. In isolated testing, the engine detects 84.4% of designed clinical discrepancies with 86.7% safety-critical recall. By coupling extraction and reconciliation evaluation on the same data, we directly quantify a 13.6% error cascade, tracing the degradation to clinical details lost during memory extraction from unstructured conversation rather than to downstream classification errors. These findings establish that validating patient-reported memories against clinical records is both feasible and necessary for safe deployment of longitudinal health agents.

</details>


### [176] [Co-Evolving Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27083)
*Naibin Gu,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Chuanyu Qin,Dingyu Yao,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoPD方法，通过并行训练专家并在RLVR过程中进行双向在线策略蒸馏，实现多能力融合，在文本、图像和视频推理任务上超越混合RLVR和MOPD等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR和OPD范式在整合多专家能力时存在能力损失：混合RLVR遭受跨能力发散问题；流水线式先训练专家再执行OPD则因师生行为模式差异大而无法充分吸收教师能力。

Method: CoPD（协同进化策略蒸馏）：并行训练专家，在每个专家进行RLVR训练过程中（而非完成后）引入OPD，专家互为教师实现双向蒸馏，共同进化以保持行为模式一致性和互补知识。

Result: 在文本、图像、视频推理任务上实现全能一体化集成，显著优于混合RLVR和MOPD，甚至超越领域特定专家模型。

Conclusion: CoPD的模型并行训练模式可能启发新的训练扩展范式。

Abstract: RLVR and OPD have become standard paradigms for post-training. We provide a unified analysis of these two paradigms in consolidating multiple expert capabilities into a single model, identifying capability loss in different ways: mixed RLVR suffers from inter-capability divergence cost, while the pipeline of first training experts and then performing OPD, though avoiding divergence, fails to fully absorb teacher capabilities due to large behavioral pattern gaps between teacher and student. We propose Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD), which encourages parallel training of experts and introduces OPD during each expert's ongoing RLVR training rather than after complete expert training, with experts serving as mutual teachers (making OPD bidirectional) to co-evolve. This enables more consistent behavioral patterns among experts while maintaining sufficient complementary knowledge throughout. Experiments validate that CoPD achieves all-in-one integration of text, image, and video reasoning capabilities, significantly outperforming strong baselines such as mixed RLVR and MOPD, and even surpassing domain-specific experts. The model parallel training pattern offered by CoPD may inspire a novel training scaling paradigm.

</details>


### [177] [AdaBFL: Multi-Layer Defensive Adaptive Aggregation for Bzantine-Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27434)
*Zehui Tang,Yuchen Liu,Feihu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于三层防御机制的自适应聚合方法AdaBFL，用于拜占庭鲁棒的联邦学习，能够动态调整防御算法权重以抵御多种投毒攻击，并在非凸非独立同分布数据下证明了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习易受投毒攻击，现有拜占庭鲁棒方法难以对多种攻击类型提供均衡防御，或依赖服务器端拥有数据集。

Method: 提出AdaBFL方法，采用新颖的三层防御机制，自适应调整防御算法权重以应对复杂攻击。

Result: 在多个数据集上的综合实验表明AdaBFL优于对比算法。

Conclusion: AdaBFL能有效解决现有拜占庭鲁棒方法在多攻击防御不均衡及依赖服务器数据集的问题，并在非凸非独立同分布数据下具有收敛保证。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is a popular distributed learning paradigm in machine learning, which enables multiple clients to collaboratively train models under the guidance of a server without exposing private client data. However, FL's decentralized nature makes it vulnerable to poisoning attacks, where malicious clients can submit corrupted models to manipulate the system. To counter such attacks, although various Byzantine-robust methods have been proposed, these methods struggle to provide balanced defense against multiple types of attacks or rely on possessing the dataset in the server. To deal with these drawbacks, thus, we propose an effective multi-layer defensive adaptive aggregation for Bzantine-robust federated learning (AdaBFL) based on a novel three-layer defensive mechanism, which can adaptively adjust the weights of defense algorithms to counter complex attacks. Moreover, we provide convergence properties of our AdaBFL method under the non-convex setting on non-iid data. Comprehensive experiments across multiple datasets validate the superiority of our AdaBFL over the comparable algorithms.

</details>


### [178] [AutoSP: Unlocking Long-Context LLM Training Via Compiler-Based Sequence Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27089)
*Ahan Gupta,Zhihao Wang,Neel Dani,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSP是首个自动优化LLM长上下文训练的系统，通过自动序列并行和长上下文感知的激活检查点技术，在NVIDIA和AMD硬件上分别将训练上下文长度提升至2.7倍和2.5倍，且运行时性能损失可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练库主要针对大参数模型优化（如ZeRO-3/FSDP、张量并行和流水线并行），缺乏对长上下文训练的高层抽象支持。用户需要手动组合序列并行等多种复杂优化技术，这需要深厚的专业知识且降低开发效率。

Method: AutoSP提出自动化解决方案，包含两个核心优化：1) 自动序列并行（automated sequence parallelism），自动编译模型并应用序列并行策略；2) 长上下文感知的激活检查点（long-context aware activation-checkpointing），针对长上下文场景优化激活内存管理。

Result: 在NVIDIA硬件上训练上下文长度提升至2.7倍，在AMD硬件上提升至2.5倍，且相比手动编写的高性能基线方案，运行时性能损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: AutoSP有效解决了长上下文LLM训练中优化复杂度过高的问题，提供自动化方案显著提升训练上下文长度，且跨平台（NVIDIA和AMD）适用，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Large-language-models (LLMs) demonstrate enormous utility in long-context tasks which require processing prompts that consist of tens to hundreds of thousands of tokens. However, existing LLM training libraries do not provide easy to use abstractions to optimize for long-context training, instead focusing on optimizations for models with large parameter counts through ZeRO-3/FSDP, Tensor and Pipeline parallelism. This forces users to rewrite LLM training libraries to incorporate compositions of various complex long-context optimizations, such as sequence-parallelism, to training pipelines; a process that requires in-depth expertise, reducing developer productivity. To tackle these challenges, we introduce AutoSP: the first automated solution to automatically optimize LLM training for longer-contexts. AutoSP compiles models and applies a targeted set of optimizations: automated sequence parallelism, and long-context aware activation-checkpointing, to drastically enhance LLM trainability at negligible cost to throughput. Our evaluation demonstrates AutoSP's capability on both NVIDIA and AMD hardware, increasing training contexts by upto 2.7$\times$ and 2.5$\times$ respectively over competitive hand-written baseline at negligible cost to runtime performance.

</details>


### [179] [Low Rank Adaptation for Adversarial Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27487)
*Han Liu,Shanghao Shi,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,Chongjie Zhang,Ning Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和实验验证，发现对抗扰动具有与LoRA类似的低秩结构，并利用这一特性将黑盒对抗攻击的搜索限制在低维子空间中，显著提升了攻击效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受LoRA中模型更新具有低秩结构的启发，探究对抗扰动是否也具备类似低秩特性，并利用该特性改善黑盒对抗攻击中查询次数过多的问题。

Method: 两步法：1) 使用参考模型和辅助数据将梯度投影到低维子空间；2) 将黑盒攻击的扰动搜索限制在该低秩子空间内，提升攻击效率。

Result: 在多种攻击方法、模型架构、数据集和威胁模型下，低秩对抗攻击相比传统方法在性能上取得了一致且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 对抗扰动本质具有低秩结构，利用该特性可以有效改进黑盒对抗攻击的效率与效果。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), which leverages the insight that model updates typically reside in a low-dimensional space, has significantly improved the training efficiency of Large Language Models (LLMs) by updating neural network layers using low-rank matrices. Since the generation of adversarial examples is an optimization process analogous to model training, this naturally raises the question: Do adversarial perturbations exhibit a similar low-rank structure?
  In this paper, we provide both theoretical analysis and extensive empirical investigation across various attack methods, model architectures, and datasets to show that adversarial perturbations indeed possess an inherently low-rank structure. This insight opens up new opportunities for improving both adversarial attacks and defenses. We mainly focus on leveraging this low-rank property to improve the efficiency and effectiveness of black-box adversarial attacks, which often suffer from excessive query requirements. Our method follows a two-step approach. First, we use a reference model and auxiliary data to guide the projection of gradients into a low-dimensional subspace. Next, we confine the perturbation search in black-box attacks to this low-rank subspace, significantly improving the efficiency and effectiveness of the adversarial attacks.
  We evaluated our approach across a range of attack methods, benchmark models, datasets, and threat models. The results demonstrate substantial and consistent improvements in the performance of our low-rank adversarial attacks compared to conventional methods.

</details>


### [180] [Anomaly Detection in Soil Heavy Metal Contamination Using Unsupervised Learning for Environmental Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27102)
*Isaac Tettey Adjokatse,Samuel Senyo Koranteng,George Yamoah Afrifa,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Marcellin Atemkeng,Joseph Bremang Tandoh,Kow Ahor Essel-Yorke,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究运用无监督机器学习框架（Isolation Forest、PCA重构误差、DBSCAN及共识方法）识别加纳中部地区12个垃圾场土壤中重金属污染异常模式，发现6个稳健异常样本（7.7%）集中在单一站点S3，异常样本的平均危害指数（HI）比正常样本高出约70-80%，并识别出三种异常类型（极端Cu富集、低Ni异常、多金属Pb-Zn共升），证明无监督学习可提供超越聚合指数的颗粒化客观洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加纳快速城市化区域中，未受监管的垃圾场导致重金属土壤污染持续威胁环境和公共健康，现有聚合健康风险指数缺乏对异常污染模式的精细辨识能力。

Method: 采集78个土壤样本（12个垃圾站点+住宅对照），分析8种重金属（As, Cd, Cr, Cu, Hg, Ni, Pb, Zn）浓度及HI、ILCR健康风险指数；分别运用Isolation Forest、PCA重构误差、DBSCAN进行异常检测，再通过共识方法综合识别稳健异常。

Result: Isolation Forest和PCA重构误差各识别12个异常（15.4%），DBSCAN未检测到噪声点；共识方法识别6个稳健异常（7.7%）全部集中在站点S3。异常样本HI均值高出正常样本70-80%，PCA重构误差与HI强正相关（r≈0.8）。识别三种异常类型：S3极端Cu富集、S4/S5异常低Ni、S9-S12中等Pb-Zn共升。

Conclusion: 无监督机器学习能够提供超越聚合健康风险指数的颗粒化客观见解，有助于实现靶向场地优先级排序和风险知情环境管理。

Abstract: Soil contamination by heavy metals poses a persistent environmental and public health concern in rapidly urbanising regions of Ghana, particularly at unregulated waste disposal sites. This study applies an unsupervised machine learning framework to detect and characterise anomalous heavy metal contamination patterns in soils from twelve waste sites and residential controls in the Central Region, of Ghana. Concentrations of eight metals (As, Cd, Cr, Cu, Hg, Ni, Pb, Zn) were analysed alongside standard health risk indices, including the Hazard Index (HI) and Incremental Lifetime Cancer Risk (ILCR). Isolation Forest and PCA reconstruction error each identified $12$ anomalous samples ($15.4\%$ of $78$ samples), while DBSCAN detected no density-isolated noise points. A consensus approach isolated six robust anomalies ($7.7\%)$, all spatially concentrated at a single site (S3). Anomalies exhibited approximately $70$--$80\%$ higher mean HI values than normal samples, with all consensus anomalies exceeding the HI$=1$ threshold. PCA reconstruction error showed a strong positive association with HI ($r \approx 0.8$), indicating consistency between multivariate deviation and health risk. Three distinct anomaly types were identified: extreme Cu enrichment at S3, anomalously low Ni at S4/S5, and moderate multi-metal (Pb--Zn) co-elevation at S9--S12. The results demonstrate that unsupervised machine learning provides granular, objective insight beyond aggregate indices, enabling targeted site prioritisation and risk-informed environmental management.

</details>


### [181] [Better Models, Faster Training: Sigmoid Attention for single-cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27124)
*Vijay Sadashivaiah,Georgios Dasoulas,Judith Mueller,Soumya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用sigmoid注意力机制替代softmax注意力机制来训练生物基础模型，发现sigmoid注意力在六个单细胞数据集上实现了25%更高的细胞类型分离度、更好的细胞类型内聚性指标和更低的验证损失，训练速度提升高达10%，并且由于全局有界导数（≤0.25）和对角雅可比结构而更加稳定。在1.6亿参数模型、无梯度裁剪的8K序列压力测试中，softmax梯度爆炸四个数量级而sigmoid保持稳定。开源TritonSigmoid GPU内核在H100上达到515 TFLOPS，性能优于FlashAttention-2和FlashSigmoid。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练稳定的生物基础模型需要重新思考注意力机制，因为softmax注意力存在固有的不稳定性问题，阻碍了大规模生物序列模型的有效训练。

Method: 使用sigmoid注意力作为softmax注意力的直接替代品，通过理论分析（全局有界导数≤0.25、对角雅可比结构）和高效GPU实现（TritonSigmoid内核，支持原生padding，在H100上达515 TFLOPS）来验证其有效性。

Result: 在六个单细胞数据集上，sigmoid注意力实现25%更高的细胞类型分离度、更好的细胞类型内聚性和更低的验证损失；训练速度提升10%；在1.6亿参数双向注意力模型、8K序列、无梯度裁剪的压力测试中，softmax梯度爆炸四个数量级而sigmoid保持稳定。

Conclusion: Sigmoid注意力在理论上是合理的，经验上优于softmax注意力，特别适合生物基础模型的训练，具备更好的表征、更快的训练速度和更高的训练稳定性。

Abstract: Training stable biological foundation models requires rethinking attention mechanisms: we find that using sigmoid attention as a drop in replacement for softmax attention a) produces better learned representations: on six diverse single-cell datasets, sigmoid achieves 25% higher cell-type separation, better cell-type cohesion metrics, and lower validation loss, b) faster training, models with sigmoid attention train up to 10% faster than their softmax counterparts, and c) more stable training by eliminating inherent sources of instability in softmax attention. We establish that sigmoid attention has globally bounded derivatives ($\leq 0.25$) as opposed to softmax, and a diagonal Jacobian structure in contrast with softmax's dense coupling, which together help alleviate training instabilities. In stress tests on 160M-parameter bidirectional attention models trained without gradient clipping on 8K-token sequences, softmax diverges catastrophically, with gradients exploding by four orders of magnitude, while sigmoid remains stable. Finally, we implement and open-source TritonSigmoid, an efficient GPU kernel that achieves 515 TFLOPS on H100 GPUs, outperforming both FlashAttention-2 and FlashSigmoid, with native padding support, which is essential for biological sequences. Our results establish sigmoid attention as both theoretically grounded and empirically superior for biological foundation models. Code is available at https://github.com/MSDLLCpapers/triton-sigmoid

</details>


### [182] [How to Guide Your Flow: Few-Step Alignment via Flow Map Reward Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27147)
*Jerry Y. Huang,Justin Lin,Sheel Shah,Kartik Nair,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Flow Map Reward Guidance (FMRG)，将引导问题重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，利用流图实现无需训练、单轨迹的生成引导框架，在仅需3次NFE的情况下，在逆问题、风格迁移、人类偏好和VLM奖励等任务上达到或超越基线，相比此前最先进方法加速至少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导方法要么需要昂贵的多粒子、多步方案，要么依赖理解不充分的近似，缺乏高效且理论清晰的框架。

Method: 将引导重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，得到一个层次化算法体系；基于流图（flow map）在最优解中的自然出现，提出FMRG方法，利用流图同时进行积分和引导，实现无需训练、单轨迹的生成框架。

Result: 在文本到图像尺度上，FMRG在逆问题、风格迁移、人类偏好和VLM奖励任务中匹配或超越基线，仅需3次NFE，相比此前最先进方法实现至少一个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: 通过最优控制视角统一了现有引导方法，并利用流图实现了高效、训练自由的单轨迹引导框架，大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: In generative modeling, we often wish to produce samples that maximize a user-specified reward such as aesthetic quality or alignment with human preferences, a problem known as guidance. Despite their widespread use, existing guidance methods either require expensive multi-particle, many-step schemes or rely on poorly understood approximations. We reformulate guidance as a deterministic optimal control problem, yielding a hierarchy of algorithms that subsumes existing approaches at the coarsest level. We show that the flow map, an object of significant recent interest for its role in fast inference, arises naturally in the optimal solution. Based on this observation, we propose Flow Map Reward Guidance (FMRG): a training-free, single-trajectory framework that uses the flow map to both integrate and guide the flow. At text-to-image scale, FMRG matches or surpasses baselines across inverse problems, style transfer, human preferences, and VLM rewards with as few as 3 NFEs, giving at least an order-of-magnitude speedup in comparison to prior state of the art.

</details>


### [183] [Generalizing the Geometry of Model Merging Through Frechet Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27155)
*Marvin F. da Silva,Mohammed Adnan,Felix Dangel,Sageev Oore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将模型合并视为流形上的Fréchet平均，通过选择对称不变性的几何结构实现感知对称的合并，并针对LoRA适配器提出了一种基于商流形的实用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法（如参数空间平均）对架构对称性敏感，因为其几何结构未考虑对称性，导致合并结果脆弱。需要一种对称性感知的合并框架。

Method: 将模型合并形式化为Fréchet平均——在适当流形上最小化测地距离之和。核心设计选择是整体几何（度量、流形、距离近似）。通过简化假设可包含Fisher合并。针对LoRA，利用其对称性导出的商流形几何，提出实用算法。

Result: Fréchet平均结合简化假设可恢复Fisher合并。针对LoRA商流形几何的实用算法与其他常用方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 模型合并不仅几何结构需对称不变，平均过程本身也必须对称不变。Fréchet平均框架为此提供了通用解，LoRA场景下的商流形方法展示了具体应用。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine multiple models into one without additional training. Naïve parameter-space averaging can be fragile under architectural symmetries, as their geometry does not take them into account. In this work we show that not only the geometry, but also the averaging procedure itself, must be symmetry-invariant to achieve symmetry-aware merges. Consequently, we propose a general solution: merging as Fréchet averaging, i.e., selecting parameters that minimize a sum of geodesic distances on an appropriate manifold. In this view, the key design choice is the overall geometry, i.e., the choice of metric, manifold, and distance approximation, that determines what it means for two models to be "close". We show that Fréchet averaging, combined with simplifying assumptions, contains Fisher merging. Building on this, we examine the particular case of low-rank adapters (LoRA), whose symmetries induce a distinct geometry: that of a quotient manifold. We outline the limitations of current LoRA merging methods, propose a practical algorithm for this setting, and show how they compare with other commonly used approaches.

</details>


### [184] [Distributional Alignment Games for Answer-Level Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27166)
*Mehryar Mohri,Jon Schneider,Yifan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于博弈论的分布对齐框架，将答案级微调（ALFT）中难以处理的边缘化问题转化为可解的对齐问题，并证明纳什均衡等价于原始优化问题的解，同时该框架统一了多样性与自改进方法，并提出了Coherence-GRPO等高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接优化答案级目标需要在大规模潜在推理路径空间上进行边缘化，这在计算上是难以处理的。

Method: 提出分布对齐博弈框架，将ALFT形式化为策略（生成器）与目标（辅助分布）之间的两人博弈，通过变分视角将难以处理的边缘化问题转化为可解的投影问题。

Result: 该框架统一了多样性与自改进（连贯性）方法，并与GRPO兼容，提出的Coherence-GRPO在数学推理任务中取得了显著的复杂度增益。

Conclusion: 博弈论框架为答案级微调提供了理论基础和高效算法，纳什均衡恰好对应原始优化问题的解。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of \emph{Answer-Level Fine-Tuning} (ALFT), where the goal is to optimize a language model based on the correctness or properties of its final answers, rather than the specific reasoning traces used to produce them. Directly optimizing answer-level objectives is computationally intractable due to the need to marginalize over the vast space of latent reasoning paths. To overcome this, we propose a general game-theoretical framework that lifts the problem to a \emph{Distributional Alignment Game}. We formulate ALFT as a two-player game between a Policy (the generator) and a Target (an auxiliary distribution). We prove that the Nash Equilibrium of this game corresponds exactly to the solution of the original answer-level optimization problem. This variational perspective transforms the intractable marginalization problem into a tractable projection problem. We demonstrate that this framework unifies recent approaches to diversity and self-improvement (coherence) and provide efficient algorithms compatible with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), such as Coherence-GRPO, yielding significant complexity gains in mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [185] [Preserving Temporal Dynamics in Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27182)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MCMC的模型无关框架，通过强制保持相邻时间点的经验转移统计一致性，来修正条件生成模型在序列生成中累积的分布偏移和时间动态失真，从而提升多元时间序列生成的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列生成方法（如GANs）仅关注边际数据分布匹配，忽略了原始多元时间序列中固有的时间动态，导致生成序列出现分布偏移和时间漂移，降低了合成序列的保真度。

Method: 提出一种模型无关的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）框架，通过强制合成序列与原始数据在相邻时间点上的经验转移统计量保持一致，来纠正条件生成模型在自回归生成过程中累积的偏差。

Result: 在Lorenz、Licor、ETTh和ILI四个数据集上，使用RCGAN、GCWGAN、TimeGAN、SigCWGAN和AECGAN五种生成模型，所提MCMC框架均一致提升了自相关对齐、偏度误差、峰度误差、R²、判别分数和预测分数等指标。

Conclusion: 高保真合成时间序列需要显式保持转移定律（transition laws），而非仅仅依赖对抗性分布匹配，为改进时间序列生成建模提供了原则性方向。

Abstract: Time-series data augmentation plays a crucial role in regression-oriented forecasting tasks, where limited data restricts the performance of deep learning models. While Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) have shown promise in synthetic time-series generation, existing approaches primarily focus on matching marginal data distributions and often overlook the temporal dynamics that naturally exist in the original multivariate time series. When generating multivariate time series, this mismatch leads to distribution shift and temporal drift, thereby degrading the fidelity of the synthetic sequences.
  In this work, we propose a model-agnostic Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC)-based framework to mitigate distribution shift and preserve temporal dynamics in synthetic time series. We provide a theoretical analysis of how conditional generative models accumulate deviations under sequential generation and demonstrate that the MCMC algorithm can correct these discrepancies by enforcing consistency with empirical transition statistics between neighboring time points.
  Extensive experiments on the Lorenz, Licor, ETTh, and ILI datasets using RCGAN, GCWGAN, TimeGAN, SigCWGAN, and AECGAN demonstrate that the proposed MCMC framework consistently improves autocorrelation alignment, skewness error, kurtosis error, R$^2$, discriminative score, and predictive score. These results suggest that synthetic time series consistent with the original data require explicit preservation of transition laws rather than solely relying on adversarial distribution matching, thereby offering a principled direction for improving generative modeling of time-series data.

</details>


### [186] [Remaining Useful Life Estimation for Turbofan Engines: A Comparative Study of Classical, CNN, and LSTM Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27234)
*Astitva Goel,Samarth Galchar,Sumit Kanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在C-MAPSS数据集上比较了经典机器学习模型（Ridge回归、Polynomial Ridge、XGBoost）与深度学习模型（1D CNN、LSTM）的RUL估计性能。单层LSTM在FD001和FD003上分别取得14.93和14.20的RMSE，优于已有文献中的深层LSTM；XGBoost在FD003上取得13.36的RMSE，展示了非线性模型的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RUL估计是PHM的关键，现有方法缺乏统一预处理下的系统比较，本文旨在公平对比各类机器学习方法的RUL估计性能。

Method: 采用统一预处理流程，在C-MAPSS FD001和FD003子集上对比Ridge回归、Polynomial Ridge、XGBoost、1D CNN和LSTM五种模型，其中深度学习模型使用原始序列，经典模型使用手工特征。

Result: 单层LSTM在FD001和FD003上RMSE为14.93和14.20，优于深层LSTM的16.14和16.18；1D CNN分别为16.97和15.68；XGBoost在FD003上达13.36。

Conclusion: 简化架构的单层LSTM在RUL估计上可超越深层结构，XGBoost等经典非线性模型在工程特征下仍具竞争力，表明模型复杂度与性能并非单调正相关。

Abstract: Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimation is a critical component of Prognostics and Health Management (PHM), enabling proactive maintenance scheduling and reducing unplanned failures in industrial equipment. This paper presents a comparative study of machine learning approaches for RUL estimation on the NASA C-MAPSS turbofan engine dataset: classical baselines (Ridge Regression, Polynomial Ridge, and XGBoost), a 1D Convolutional Neural Network (CNN), and a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network. All models are evaluated on the FD001 and FD003 subsets under an identical preprocessing pipeline to ensure a fair comparison. Among raw-sequence models, the LSTM achieves RMSE of 14.93 and 14.20 on FD001 and FD003 respectively, outperforming the deep LSTM reported by Zheng et al.~\cite{paper} (RMSE 16.14 and 16.18) despite using a simpler single-layer architecture. The 1D CNN achieves RMSE of 16.97 on FD001 and 15.68 on FD003, demonstrating competitive performance on FD003 while producing more conservative RUL predictions on FD001. Ridge Regression is evaluated on raw and engineered features, while other classical models use only engineered inputs. XGBoost achieves an RMSE of 13.36 on FD003, highlighting the competitiveness of nonlinear modeling.

</details>


### [187] [Analytical Correction for Subsampling Bias in Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27239)
*Jiaru Zhang,Zeyun Deng,Juanwu Lu,Ziran Wang,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分析性偏差校正（ABC）方法，通过闭式调整修正漂移模型中minibatch质心的O(1/n)偏差，将偏差降至O(1/n^2)且不增加方差，仅需两行代码即可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移模型（Drifting models）在训练中仅能使用minibatch样本估计softmax加权质心，但该经验估计存在O(1/n)的偏差，影响模型生成质量。

Method: 推导出softmax自归一化导致的偏差高阶项，利用批内统计量近似领先偏差项，提出闭式解析偏差校正（ABC）公式。

Result: 理论上证明ABC将偏差从O(1/n)降至O(1/n^2)，不导致一阶方差增加，保持凸包包含性；实验中在CIFAR-10上降低FID并加速训练，小n时增益最显著。

Conclusion: ABC是一种轻量高效、理论完备的偏差校正方法，可无缝集成到漂移模型中提升生成质量。

Abstract: Drifting models are capable one-step generative models trained to follow a drifting field. The field combines attractive and repulsive softmax-weighted centroids over the data and current-generator distributions. In practice, only a minibatch of $n$ samples from each distribution is available, and each centroid is approximated by an empirical estimate. In this paper, we begin by showing that the minibatch centroid is in general a biased estimator of the target centroid, with a pointwise $O(1/n)$ bias arising from softmax self-normalization. Correcting this bias requires the expectation over the full distribution, which is intractable. We instead approximate the leading bias term from in-batch statistics and propose Analytical Bias Correction (ABC), a closed-form plug-in adjustment. We prove that ABC reduces the bias from $O(1/n)$ to $O(1/n^2)$, introduces no first-order increase in total variance, and preserves convex-hull containment of the corrected centroid. In practice, ABC requires only two additional lines of code and has negligible wall-time overhead under compiled execution. Toy experiments confirm the theoretical $O(1/n)$ and $O(1/n^2)$ scaling. On CIFAR-10, ABC reduces FID and trains faster, with the largest gains at small $n$, where the bias is most significant.

</details>


### [188] [AutoREC: A software platform for developing reinforcement learning agents for equivalent circuit model generation from electrochemical impedance spectroscopy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27266)
*Ali Jaberi,Yonatan Kurniawan,Robert Black,Shayan Mousavi M.,Kabir Verma,Zoya Sadighi,Santiago Miret,Jason Hattrick-Simpers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍AutoREC——一个开源的Python包，利用深度强化学习（Double DQN with prioritized experience replay）从EIS数据自动生成等效电路模型（ECM），在合成数据集上成功率超99.6%，并能泛化到电池、腐蚀、OER和CO₂还原等未见实验系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ECM构建依赖人工试错，需要领域专家且难以规模化，尤其不适合自驾驶实验室等自主实验流程。AutoREC旨在将ECM构建自动化，消除人工瓶颈。

Method: 将ECM构建建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中的序列决策问题；采用Double Deep Q-Network（DDQN）结合优先经验回放（prioritized experience replay）进行训练；设计了专门的死循环缓解策略（dead-loop mitigation strategy）以应对复杂动作空间中的无效回路。

Result: 训练后的RL agent在合成数据集上成功率超过99.6%，能泛化到电池、腐蚀、析氧反应（OER）和CO₂还原系统等未见过的真实EIS数据，展现了强泛化能力。

Conclusion: AutoREC有望作为自适应数据驱动ECM生成平台，可集成到自动化电化学工作流中，同时作者也讨论了当前局限及未来改进策略。

Abstract: This paper introduces AutoREC, an open-source Python package for developing reinforcement learning (RL) agents to automatically generate equivalent circuit models (ECMs) from electrochemical impedance spectroscopy (EIS) data. While ECMs are a standard framework for interpreting EIS data, traditional identification is typically based on manual trial-and-error, which requires domain experts and limits scalability, particularly in autonomous experimental pipelines such as self-driving laboratories. AutoREC addresses this challenge by formulating ECM construction as a sequential decision-making problem within a Markov Decision Process framework. It implements a Double Deep Q-Network with prioritized experience replay, along with a dedicated dead-loop mitigation strategy, to efficiently explore a complex action space for circuit generation. To demonstrate the capabilities of the platform, we trained an RL agent using AutoREC and evaluated its strengths and limitations across diverse datasets, while also discussing possible strategies to mitigate these limitations in future agent designs. The trained agent achieved a success rate exceeding $99.6\%$ on synthetic datasets and demonstrated strong generalization to unseen experimental EIS data from batteries, corrosion, oxygen evolution reaction, and CO$_2$ reduction systems. These results position AutoREC as a promising platform for adaptive and data-driven ECM generation, with potential for integration into automated electrochemical workflows.

</details>


### [189] [Predicting Covariate-Driven Spatial Deformation for Nonstationary Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27280)
*Minghao Gu,Weizhi Lin,Qiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于协变量驱动的空间变形方法，通过李代数截断理论实现非平稳高斯过程在协变量新条件下的可预测建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间变形方法是一种静态方法，无法考虑协变量引起的空间相关性变化，因而不能在新协变量条件下对非平稳高斯过程进行预测。

Method: 将空间变形建模为协变量的函数，通过速度场（位于李代数中）生成微分同胚变形。证明了一般李代数中多个协变量高阶交互项可在适度物理假设下截断，从而建立简洁的协变量驱动变形函数形式，并开发了高效的估计-推断算法。

Result: 通过模拟研究和两个案例（制造业与地统计学）验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法使空间变形方法具备了在协变量新条件下进行非平稳GP预测的能力，理论上的截断结果保障了估计的稳定性与可行性。

Abstract: Nonstationary Gaussian processes (GPs) are essential for modeling complex, locally heterogeneous spatial data. A common modeling approach is the spatial deformation method that warps the domain to recover isotropy. However, this static method does not account for changes in spatial correlation induced by covariates, limiting its ability to predict nonstationary GPs under new covariate conditions.
  To enable predictive modeling of the deformation method, we propose to model the spatial deformation as a function of covariates. The spaces of diffeomorphic deformations and Euclidean covariate vectors are connected by characterizing deformations as generated by velocity fields living in a Lie algebra. To overcome the estimation instability caused by high-order interactions between multiple covariates in a general Lie algebra, we prove that those interactions can be truncated with a moderate physical assumption. Based on the theoretical results, a concise functional form of deformations driven by multiple covariates can be established, and an efficient estimation-inference algorithm is developed for out-of-sample nonstationary GP prediction with limited covariate-deformation sample pairs. The effectiveness and generalizability of the method are demonstrated on a simulation study and two case studies, in the fields of manufacturing and geostatistics, respectively.

</details>


### [190] [PINN-Cast: Exploring the Role of Continuous-Depth NODE in Transformers and Physics Informed Loss as Soft Physical Constraints in Short-term Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27313)
*Hira Saleem,Flora Salim,Cormac Purcell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种连续深度Transformer编码器用于天气预报，将神经ODE动态集成到每个编码块中，并引入双分支注意力模块和物理信息训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值天气预报计算成本高且流程复杂；现有Transformer预报器缺乏物理先验且离散层更新不适合建模平滑的潜在动力学。

Method: 1) 用基于ODE的更新（自适应数值积分）替代离散残差更新；2) 双分支注意力模块：常规patch-wise自注意力 + 对注意力logits施加导数算子的辅助分支；3) 物理信息训练目标作为软约束。

Result: 与标准离散Transformer基线和现有连续时间神经ODE变体相比，PINN-Cast在短期天气预报中表现出重要性。

Conclusion: 连续深度编码器结合物理信息约束能有效提升数据驱动天气预报的性能。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction has long relied on physics-based numerical weather prediction (NWP), whose accuracy comes at the cost of substantial compute and complex simulation workflows. Recent transformer-based forecasters offer efficient data-driven alternatives, however transformers are physics-agnostic models. Additionally, standard transformer encoders evolve representations through discrete layer updates that may be less suited to modeling smooth latent dynamics. In this work, we propose a continuous-depth transformer encoder for weather forecasting that integrates Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (Neural ODE) dynamics within each encoder block. Specifically, we replace discrete residual updates with ODE-based updates solved using adaptive numerical integration. We also introduce a two-branch attention module that combines conventional patch-wise self-attention with an auxiliary branch that applies a derivative operator to attention logits, providing an additional change-sensitive interaction signal. To further align forecasts with governing principles, we propose a customized physics-informed training objective that enforces physical consistency as a soft constraint. We evaluate the proposed method against a standard discrete transformer baseline and an existing continuous-time Neural ODE forecasting variant, demonstrating the importance of PINN-Cast in short term weather forecasting.

</details>


### [191] [TypeBandit: Type-Level Context Allocation and Reweighting for Effective Attribute Completion in Heterogeneous Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27356)
*Ta-Yang Wang,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor Prasanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TypeBandit，一种轻量级、模型无关的异构图属性补全方法，通过拓扑感知初始化、类型级Bandit采样和联合表示学习，在有限采样预算下解决节点类型间信息不对称问题，并在多个基准数据集上取得有效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构图广泛用于建模多关系系统，但缺失节点属性是下游学习的主要瓶颈。论文识别并形式化了类型依赖的信息不对称现象：不同节点类型在属性补全中提供的有用信号水平存在显著差异，现有方法未能有效处理这一挑战。

Method: TypeBandit包含三个核心组件：1）拓扑感知初始化（结合结构度先验与特征传播的混合预训练方案）；2）类型级Bandit采样（在全局采样预算下自适应分配各类型的采样配额）；3）联合表示学习（利用采样得到的类型摘要作为共享上下文信号构建表示）。该方法在类型层面而非目标节点局部邻域操作，保持自适应状态紧凑高效。

Result: 在DBLP、IMDB、ACM上的固定划分协议下取得数据集相关但实际有效的增益；消融实验、稳定性、效率、语义传播和OGBN-MAG采样实验进一步验证了TypeBandit在类型信息分布不均且采样资源受限场景下的实用性。

Conclusion: TypeBandit作为一种实用的异构图属性补全策略，具有良好的架构灵活性（可作为R-GCN、HetGNN、HGT、SimpleHGN等代表性异构GNN骨干的前端），特别适用于类型特定信息分布不均且采样资源有限的场景。

Abstract: Heterogeneous graphs are widely used to model multi-relational systems, but missing node attributes remain a major bottleneck for downstream learning. In this paper, we identify and formalize type-dependent information asymmetry: the phenomenon that different node types provide substantially different levels of useful signal for attribute completion. Motivated by this observation, we propose TypeBandit, a lightweight, model-agnostic methodology for heterogeneous attribute completion. TypeBandit combines topology-aware initialization, type-level bandit sampling, and joint representation learning. It allocates a finite global sampling budget across node types, samples representative nodes within each type, and uses the resulting sampled type summaries as shared contextual signals during representation construction. By operating at the type level rather than over each target node's local neighborhood, TypeBandit keeps the adaptive state compact and practical for large heterogeneous graphs.
  A key advantage of TypeBandit is architectural flexibility. Rather than requiring a new heterogeneous graph neural network architecture, TypeBandit acts as a type-aware front end for representative heterogeneous GNN backbones, including R-GCN, HetGNN, HGT, and SimpleHGN. We further introduce a hybrid pretraining scheme that combines structural degree priors with feature propagation, yielding a more reliable initializer than degree-only pretraining. Under a fixed-split protocol on DBLP, IMDB, and ACM, TypeBandit provides dataset-dependent but practically meaningful gains. Additional ablation, stability, efficiency, semantic-propagation, and sampled OGBN-MAG experiments support TypeBandit as a practical strategy for heterogeneous attribute completion when type-specific information is unevenly distributed and sampling resources are limited.

</details>
